# W2 cold-chain technical design â€” 2026-10-17

**Decision:** `D-2026-10-17-w2-techdesign-cold`. Architect contract for W2-00 and W2-01..04; Planner sequences and tests these choices. Proposed screen scope still needs owner confirmation. ## 1. W2-00: ordinary source-to-evidence format

The active authenticated path uses **SourceEnvelopeV3**, not the older staged v1/v2 envelope: v3 names record families but refuses them, while S2 also refuses non-measurements (`contracts/assetops_contracts/source_envelope_v3.py:1-43,77-78,240-246`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:268-280`). Extend that path as schema **4**, `assetops.source-envelope`; retain v3's allocated `(gateway,source,epoch,sequence)` message identity, canonical JSON, UTC instants and exact numeric text. `source_envelope.py` v1/v2 and existing Stage artifacts remain byte-for-byte readable. The v4 parser accepts v4 only; a version dispatcher accepts v3 and v4. v3 receipts, dedup keys and accepted rows are untouched. A new publication profile pins v4; never switch an existing profile in place (`contracts/assetops_contracts/source_envelope_v3.py:1-43,70-78`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:87-107`).

| v4 family / kind | Exact payload keys in addition to common v3 top-level fields | Rule |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `MEASUREMENT` | Exact v4 keys `family,measurement,value,unit,quality,unavailable_reason,session_id,connector_id`; the last two are explicit null for cold and non-session EV channels. v4 alone permits `STATE_AT_INSTANT`,`INTERVAL_MEAN`,`INTERVAL_DELTA`; delta is nonnegative `kWh` over a positive `[interval_start,interval_end)` and requires both EV IDs. Cold point Â°C uses `degC`; states use `state`; mean power uses `kW`. | Define a separate `V4_MEASUREMENTS` allowlist in `source_envelope_v4.py`; leave shared `ENVELOPE_MEASUREMENTS` and v3 parsing unchanged. Null value only with `UNAVAILABLE`; `SUSPECT` excluded from assertions. |
| `EVENT` / `DOOR_OPEN`, `DOOR_CLOSE`, `START_REQUEST`, `START_RESULT`, `TRANSFER_RESULT`, `POWER_LOSS`, `POWER_RESTORED`, `MANUAL_LOG` | `family,record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,component_id,actor,outcome,unit,value,quality`. `event_id` and `correlation_id` required identifiers; `component_id` required except Site power events; `actor` = `CONTROLLER`,`SENSOR`,`OPERATOR`; `outcome` = `REQUESTED`,`SUCCEEDED`,`FAILED`,`OPEN`,`CLOSED`,`LOST`,`RESTORED`,`OBSERVED`,`UNKNOWN`, constrained per kind; `unit/value` either both null or a permitted quantitative pair (manual log `degC` only); `quality` = `GOOD` or `SUSPECT`. | An event is a witnessed public observation, not private disturbance truth. `MANUAL_LOG` needs registered operator source and stays contextual unless calibrated to the probe role. |
| `EVENT` / `EV_SESSION_START`, `EV_SESSION_END`, `EV_SESSION_ABORT`, `EV_CONNECTOR_FAULT`, `EV_CONNECTOR_CLEAR` | Same keys plus `session_id,connector_id`; both required for session kinds, `connector_id` required for fault/clear and `session_id` nullable there. `outcome` = `STARTED`,`COMPLETED`,`ABORTED`,`FAULTED`,`CLEARED`; `value/unit` null. | Session identity scoped to `(tenant,site,charger asset,connector asset,session_id)`; no vehicle/customer identity. |

Kind-to-outcome allowlist is exact: `DOOR_OPENâ†’OPEN`, `DOOR_CLOSEâ†’CLOSED`, `START_REQUESTâ†’REQUESTED`, `START_RESULTâ†’SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `TRANSFER_RESULTâ†’SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `POWER_LOSSâ†’LOST`, `POWER_RESTOREDâ†’RESTORED`, `MANUAL_LOGâ†’OBSERVED`, `EV_SESSION_STARTâ†’STARTED`, `EV_SESSION_ENDâ†’COMPLETED`, `EV_SESSION_ABORTâ†’ABORTED`, `EV_CONNECTOR_FAULTâ†’FAULTED`, `EV_CONNECTOR_CLEARâ†’CLEARED`. All other pairs refuse `INVALID_PAYLOAD`. `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/`degC`; every other event requires both null. A record kind with no `session_id` still carries the key as null; `connector_id` is likewise explicit null for cold events.

Every v4 top-level object keeps the v3 exact keys; `message` keeps `message_id,sequence_epoch,sequence`, `source` and `mapping` keep v3 keys, `timing` keeps `source_time,published_at,interval_start,interval_end`. Events require null interval bounds; their source time is the observed transition, not a duration. For cold event `correlation_id` is the controller outage/attempt ID; a door pair shares its door-cycle ID. For EV, `correlation_id=session_id` on session events and optional registered fault episode ID on fault/clear. `event_id` is stable under retransmission and unique within `(site,source,record_kind)`; message ID remains transport identity. Canonical event payload contains explicit nulls for inapplicable fields. No arbitrary JSON detail, free text, Lab run ID, oracle, private cause, or stock identity. Event IDs generated from frozen scenario + public device channel + transition ordinal in Lab; physical adapters preserve vendor stable event ID or derive a documented hash from raw immutable event key. Cross-source event correlation is a claim input, never an acceptance dedup key.

| Boundary | Exact change / refusal |
| --- | --- |
| Parser and mapper | Add `source_envelope_v4.py` and golden vectors; keep v3 code frozen. New `simulator-lab-gateway-cold` v1 only allows the channel list in Â§4. A v4 kind under v3 is `SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED`. |
| S2 intake | Dispatch by stated schema only; validate auth scope, pinned binding revision, role/asset/kind/unit and source time within Foundation/binding validity before accept. Same message ID + same SHA is `DUPLICATE`; same message ID + different SHA remains byte-stable `MESSAGE_CONFLICT`/`CONFLICT`. Different SHA at existing `(tenant,site,source,record_kind,event_id)` is `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`/`CONFLICT`; a second distinct EV START for `(tenant,site,connector asset,session_id)` is `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`/`CONFLICT`. Each conflict sets `conflicts_with_evidence_id` to the first accepted record. Identical event under a new message ID is `DUPLICATE` pointing to that event. `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH`, `EV_DELTA_UNIT_INVALID`, `EV_DELTA_INTERVAL_INVALID`, `EV_DELTA_VALUE_INVALID` are `REJECTED`, with null conflict reference. Every refusal writes `evidence_rejection` and one ordinal disposition. Receipt V2 `conflicts` counts all three conflict kinds; `rejected` counts the four EV codes and other refusals. Distinct conflicting terminal events remain accepted for analytic contradiction (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger_service.py:79-167`; `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`). |
| Accepted storage/read | `accepted_evidence.family` becomes `MEASUREMENT|EVENT`; for EVENT, `measurement,conversion_kind` become nullable, while event quality remains `GOOD|SUSPECT`; original/normalized value and unit null unless quantitative manual log. Typed event columns store identity/kind/correlation/actor/outcome, not merely envelope JSON. Keep exact `envelope_text`, SHA, accepted-as Foundation/binding, received/published/source times and S4-05 publication lineage. `/evidence/records` adds `family,record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,session_id,connector_id,outcome` and family/kind filters; old records serialize nulls. No event is converted into a fake state reading (`backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:54-59`). |

Postgres migration head is `0019_verification_core` (`backend/migrations/versions/0019_verification_core.py:1-29`). All new rows carry `(tenant_id,site_id)` and composite same-Site FKs. Use `ledger_append_only()` for facts/revisions, and the existing S1 effective/recorded validity rules for Foundation and binding publications (`backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`; `backend/migrations/versions/0007_dispatch_assessment.py:83-125`). No data backfill invents events or observations.

`0022` adds nullable `finding.subject_asset_id uuid` with scoped asset FK; relaxes `finding.generator_asset_id` to nullable; adds CHECK `(domain='mini_grid' AND generator_asset_id IS NOT NULL) OR (domain='cold_chain' AND subject_asset_id IS NOT NULL AND generator_asset_id IS NULL)`. It also adds `finding_revision.domain text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'mini_grid'` for existing rows, drops the default after backfill, adds UNIQUE `(tenant_id,site_id,id,domain)` on `finding`, and replaces the revision's parent FK with `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id,domain)` to that unique key. Thus revision CHECKs can use their own stored domain without trusting a caller. `0025` widens both domain CHECKs with `'ev_charging'`. One literal per domain is used in Finding, FindingRevision, `recompute_job.domain`, and episode/session producers: `mini_grid`, `cold_chain`, `ev_charging`. Existing `mini_grid` rows stay untouched apart from the additive revision domain backfill. `recompute_job.domain` has no SQL CHECK. `0022` adds the `(tenant_id,site_id,domain,subject_asset_id,anchor_start)` index and updates Finding store/read selection by domain. The shared fuel consequence CHECK remains intact. The `0020` accepted-event unique index is enforced in the ledger transaction; race losers get the conflict disposition above.

For EV session events, `accepted_evidence.asset_id` is the effective bound EV_CONNECTOR UUID and `connector_id` is its canonical public identifier; S2 resolves the equality before INSERT. Every accepted-evidence INSERT, including EVENT and EV START, retains `ON CONFLICT ON CONSTRAINT accepted_evidence_message_unique DO NOTHING`. For each ordinal, the ledger opens a savepoint around that INSERT. On a message-target `DO NOTHING` or a unique violation of `accepted_event_identity_unique` or `accepted_ev_start_unique`, it rolls back to the savepoint where needed and rereads under the same batch transaction in fixed order: message key, then event identity, then EV START identity. A matching message SHA returns `DUPLICATE`; a changed message SHA returns `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` even when the event or START key also collides. If the message key is free, a matching event SHA returns `DUPLICATE` and a changed event SHA returns `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`; only if neither key matches does a distinct START collision return `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`. Thus message wins a double collision, then event wins over session. Each conflict references the first accepted row for its winning key. A non-unique or unlisted unique violation propagates; never convert it to a typed conflict. Unique indexes arbitrate races; the reread is not an application precheck.

| Existing dispatch-shaped NOT NULL field | `cold_chain` value in `0022`; `ev_charging` uses the same rule in `0025` | Constraint |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `analytic_run.cohort_status,cohort_sha256,cohort` | `NOT_APPLICABLE`, SHA-256 of canonical JSON `[]`, `[]` | Preserve NOT NULL; domain reads must never interpret this as a selected mini-grid cohort. |
| `finding.mechanism,analytic_id,anchor_start,anchor_assessment_revision_id,matcher_version,opened_at` | `COLD_EXCURSION`/`COLD_START_RESPONSE`, matching `cold-excursion/1`/`cold-start-response/1`, episode anchor, first selected assessment, `cold-episode/1`, first creation time. EV: `EV_INTERRUPTION`, `ev-session/1`, START source time, first selected assessment, `ev-session/1`, first creation time. | All remain NOT NULL. `finding.cohort_sha256` is null for both. |
| `finding_revision.episode_start,episode_end` | Nullable in `0022`. Cold: exact known bounds when both known, otherwise null; EV: START and terminal source times, terminal null while unresolved. | Replace old bound CHECK with `episode_start IS NULL OR episode_end IS NULL OR episode_start<=episode_end`; `mini_grid` CHECK requires both nonnull. |
| `finding_revision.candidate_minutes,necessary_minutes,indeterminate_minutes` | Nullable in `0022`; null for cold and EV because dispatch candidate decomposition has no meaning. | `mini_grid` CHECK requires all three nonnull and nonnegative; cold/EV CHECK requires all three null. Cold duration bounds live in `cold_episode_revision`; EV duration is derived from accepted events. |
| `finding_revision.support_sha256,cited_evidence_sha256,input_evidence_sha256,basis_sha256` | Remain NOT NULL: SHA-256 of sorted exact supporting `(evidence_id,envelope_sha256)` pairs; SHA-256 of sorted exact cited accepted pairs; `analytic_run.input_sha256`; revision basis digest, respectively. Empty support/citation hashes SHA-256 of canonical `[]`, never empty text. | Same derivation for EV and mini-grid; no private truth. |
| `finding_revision.claim,limitations,assessment_revision_id,analytic_run_id,claim_level,title,mechanism,matcher_version,analytic_id,model_version` | Remain NOT NULL; domain result/limitation JSON arrays, selected assessment/run IDs, bounded claim and domain labels/model version. | Existing scoped FKs and immutable revision rules apply. |

## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract

Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

| Role / asset type | Kind, unit, cardinality | Need |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `chamber_temperature` / `TEMPERATURE_PROBE` | point number `degC`, PER_ASSET | required, one calibrated probe per `COLD_ROOM` via declared probe-to-room link |
| `cold_policy_low`,`cold_policy_high` / `COLD_ROOM` | Foundation control number `degC`, PER_ASSET | required; low < high, same effective revision |
| `compressor_state` / `COMPRESSOR` | point state `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, PER_ASSET | required for cooling claim |
| `compressor_power` / `COMPRESSOR` | interval mean `kW`, PER_ASSET | optional corroboration |
| `grid_state` / `METER` | point state `AVAILABLE|LOST`, ONE | required for outage step |
| `backup_request`,`backup_result` / `GENERATOR` | EVENT `START_REQUEST`,`START_RESULT`, ONE per Site | required for start-response claim; `correlation_id` separates attempts |
| `generator_state` / `GENERATOR` | point state `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, ONE | required for observed running state |
| `backup_bus_state` / `AC_BUS` | point state `POWERED|UNPOWERED`, ONE | required for supply-to-cooling claim |
| `transfer_result` / `TRANSFER_SWITCH` | EVENT `TRANSFER_RESULT`, ONE per Site | optional: absent blocks switch-position claim; `correlation_id` separates attempts |
| `door_event` / `COLD_ROOM` | EVENT open/close, PER_ASSET | optional context |
| `ambient_temperature` / `TEMPERATURE_PROBE` | point number `degC`, PER_ASSET | optional context, never substitutes chamber probe |
| `manual_temperature` / `COLD_ROOM` | EVENT manual log `degC`, PER_ASSET | optional context; never silently closes a probe gap |

Foundation topology uses explicit `COLD_ROOM`â†’`TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`â†’room, busâ†’compressor, generatorâ†’transferâ†’bus relations; every referenced asset must exist, one chamber probe cannot be primary for two rooms, and cycles/duplicate edges are refused. Policy band belongs to a room and is effective for the selected window; changing it creates a Foundation revision and binding set selection, never an in-place update. Validate finite exact positive thermal parameters, `0<=hysteresis<high-low`, probe error `>=0`, timeout `>0`, and cadence 5 minutes for the first Lab profile. Publication readiness must say **â€œAdd a chamber temperature probe and bind its Â°C readingâ€**, **â€œSet a lower and upper temperature policy for this roomâ€**, **â€œBind the generator start request and resultâ€**, or **â€œTransfer position is not reported; switch state is unknownâ€** as applicable. Configuration readiness, commissioning evidence and gateway contact remain separate (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_service.py:267-317,674-715`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:32-53`).

## 4. W2-02 causal producer and publication

Add `cold-thermal/1` `ModelSpec` and `cold-publication/1`; resolve frozen profile identity in `host/execution_adapter.py:113-140`, `host/lab_execution.py:1978-2015` and `simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/execute.py:90-105` through a closed registry, with fuel/mini-grid identities preserved. Reject unknown pack/profile/parameter version before Draft. The kernel receives typed model laws and power ports, not a cold-specific branch. Lab freezes Foundation/binding IDs, scenario SHA, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity; Stage seals public v4 envelopes, registered Commit links publication, SQLite outbox sends authenticated Batch V2, and S2 alone creates accepted evidence (`host/lab_execution.py:2154-2164,2428-2501`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:55-66`).

**Time and state.** Fixed 1-minute UTC step; all updates use exact rationals/decimal text and a defined end-of-step publication order. State: chamber air temperature `T` degC, controller demand, compressor `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, grid/backup bus energized flags, generator `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, transfer position `GRID|BACKUP|OPEN`, door `OPEN|CLOSED`, probe `OK|FAILED`. Private forcing: ambient `Ta`, door schedule, grid loss, generator failure, probe failure; private truth never serializes into a product envelope. Initial `T=3Â°C`, `Ta=20Â°C`, door closed, compressor off, grid and bus on, generator stopped, transfer grid, probe OK. Per minute, `heat_kWh = (heat_leak_kW_per_C*(Ta-T) + door_open*door_heat_kWh*60)*1/60`; `cool_kWh = compressor_running_and_bus_powered*cooling_kW*1/60`; `T_next=T+(heat_kWh-cool_kWh)/thermal_capacity_kWh_per_C`. Defaults: thermal capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min` (stored as `1.2 kW` equivalent while open), cooling `3 kW`, compressor start delay 1 minute, generator start delay 2 minutes, transfer delay 1 minute, start timeout 5 minutes, control on `T>=4Â°C`, off `T<=2Â°C`, policy `[2,4]Â°C`, hysteresis 0.2Â°C, probe bound Â±0.1Â°C. Clamp no state: fail run if `T` outside `[-40,60]Â°C`, energy residual nonzero, negative capacity/power, or impossible transition. The standalone cold pack owns its generator and bus power port; W2-02 does not compose with mini-grid.

Grid loss opens transfer and deenergizes compressor bus; controller emits `START_REQUEST` only if backup enabled, generator reaches RUNNING after delay unless failed, transfer closes to BACKUP after delay if generator power exists, and compressor restarts after its own delay if controller still demands cooling. Failed start emits `START_RESULT/FAILED` at timeout; successful start emits `START_RESULT/SUCCEEDED` when running. Transfer result is emitted only if a position sensor is configured; absence stays absence. Door events are sensor transitions; door opening adds heat only while open. Probe failure emits one `UNAVAILABLE` reading with reason `SENSOR_FAILURE` if the profile publishes it, then no numeric sample; reporting-path loss emits no envelope. Device measurement is `T + fixed observation bias` (default 0), rounded to 0.1Â°C by half-even rule; no private cause field. Controller and power events use observed public transitions and stable correlation ID.

All six `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` use `cold-scenario/1`, UTC run 2026-10-06 19:00â€“20:00 with a closing-boundary sample at 20:00, seed 20417, one standalone `cold-thermal/1` pack, and no mini-grid composition. The selected evidence/assessment window is half-open `[19:00,20:05)`, containing exactly 13 scheduled five-minute points from 19:00 through 20:00; no 20:05 point is included. Assets are `room-1`, `probe-1`, `door-1`, `generator-1`, `grid-1`, `bus-1`, `compressor-1`; generator backup enabled. Base initial state is T=3.00Â°C, ambient=20Â°C, door CLOSED, compressor OFF, grid/bus POWERED, generator STOPPED, transfer GRID, probe OK; excursion-derived cases override initial T to 3.80Â°C. Each row lists the entire non-default forcing, ordered by `(time,kind,asset)`; observation faults occur after a valid world transition and cannot bypass `PhysicallyUnacceptable` guards.

| Pack | UTC forcing `(time,kind,asset/connector,value)`; door schedule | Public assertion in `[19:00,20:05)` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `normal` | `(19:05,GRID_LOSS,grid-1,LOST)`, `(19:35,GRID_RESTORE,grid-1,AVAILABLE)`; ambient override 10Â°C, door CLOSED throughout. | Request 19:05, success 19:07, transfer 19:08. On restore at 19:35 transfer returns to GRID, generator stops, bus stays powered and compressor follows controller demand. Even with no cooling, T(20:00) <3.5Â°C from 3.00Â°C; every sample has >0.5Â°C margin below the 4Â°C upper policy, so no excursion Finding. |
| `excursion` | `(19:00,GRID_LOSS,grid-1,LOST)`, `(19:00,GENERATOR_FAILURE,generator-1,FAILED)`; door CLOSED throughout; initial T=3.80Â°C. | Failed result 19:05; T at 19:15 >4.06Â°C and at 19:20 >4.15Â°C; first two GOOD out-of-band samples qualify, latest start 19:15, open episode at 20:00 has duration lower bound 45 min, exceeding 15 min. |
| `backup-failed` | Same forcing and initial state as `excursion`; `(19:10,DOOR_OPEN,door-1,OPEN)`, `(19:11,DOOR_CLOSE,door-1,CLOSED)`. | Explicit failed start plus unpowered bus and compressor establish `OBSERVED_FAILURE`; door adds 0.02 kWh (<0.017Â°C) and remains contextual, exposure Finding still qualifies. |
| `missing-probe` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation drop at scheduled points `[19:15,19:45)` (19:15 through 19:40), `PROBE_REPORT,probe-1,DROP`; door CLOSED. | Only 7 of 13 scheduled temperature points remain, coverage 7/13 <0.8; temperature claim `UNKNOWN`, no supported exposure Finding; start failure remains observed. |
| `late-reading` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation hold `(19:20,PROBE_REPORT,probe-1,RELEASE_AT_20:10)`; door CLOSED. | At first cutoff 20:05, 12/13 points remain and first two high samples are 19:15/19:25; at 20:10 the accepted 19:20 point enqueues `cold_chain` recompute, changes exact evidence basis and bounds, appends a revision without rewriting the old one. |
| `contradictory` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation fault `(19:05,START_RESULT,generator-1,SUCCEEDED)` emits an additional distinct public controller event ID beside the genuine FAILED event, both GOOD and same correlation ID; door CLOSED. | Both events are individually valid and accepted; start-response claim is `CONTRADICTORY`, never `OBSERVED_FAILURE`. The frozen observation profile, not the physical model, creates the conflicting report. |

`cold-publication/1` emits chamber `STATE_AT_INSTANT degC` every 5 minutes including end; grid/generator/compressor/bus states on transitions and 5-minute heartbeat; compressor `INTERVAL_MEAN kW` per aligned 5-minute slot; door/start/transfer/power events on transition. `simulator/assetops_simulator/observation/transform.py` owns `cold-observation/1` hold/drop/fault transform variants, zero bias and default 3-second latency before Draft. The model retains one calibrated primary chamber probe per room; delete the two-primary-probe disagreement rule. Contradictory distinct GOOD controller outcomes for one attempt cause `CONTRADICTORY`. `cold-door-heat` property unit is `kWh/min`, with 0.02 per open minute; the equation's `*60` converts it to kW before its one-minute integration. The Lab persisted-state validator uses the `cold-thermal/1` finite vocabularies above, not the mini-grid generator lifecycle vocabulary; extend the closed identity/profile registries. `Role.value_kind` adds `EVENT`, and EVENT bindings use `conversion='IDENTITY'`, with typed outcome allowlist and no numeric conversion. Foundation controls stay NUMBER, not a new `Role.value_kind`.

The closed cold mapping uses `(device_id,signal_id)`: `(chamber-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(ambient-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(grid-meter,state)` state point, `(generator-controller,state)` state point, `(compressor-controller,state)` state point, `(backup-bus-meter,state)` state point, `(compressor-meter,power)` kW interval mean, `(door-sensor,event)` door events, `(generator-controller,start-event)` request/result events, `(transfer-controller,event)` transfer result, `(grid-meter,event)` loss/restored events, `(operator-log,temperature-event)` manual log. Each tuple is a separate versioned mapping entry with only its listed kinds, values and cadence. W2-00 also reserves EV entries `(charger-controller,session-event)` for session start/end/abort and `(connector-controller,fault-event)` for fault/clear, with connector/session identity rules above; W2-05b supplies the EV pack and its own frozen mapping version. The same signal ID on a different device is not an implicit alias (`simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:22-46,87-107`).

## 5. W2-03 assessment, episodes and Findings

Analytic IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. Input selection is accepted evidence only, fixed half-open `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at` cutoff on server `received_at`/decision time, effective Foundation/binding versions, and source gate. Include exact accepted IDs and SHA, configuration IDs, model version, window/cutoff in sorted basis SHA. A reading is usable only if GOOD, calibrated, mapped to the room, and clock-attested; SUSPECT is context. Expected five-minute points: coverage = usable distinct scheduled timestamps / scheduled timestamps, capped at 1; `>=0.8` needed for an interval claim, but no gap >10 minutes may be bridged. Latest point must be <=10 minutes old at read time for a **current** state; historical window conclusion can remain bounded with explicit as-of. A failed worker or newer accepted relevant evidence marks prior projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new immutable revision is written.

Excursion begins after two consecutive GOOD observations above upper band (or below lower band) at least 5 minutes apart; return requires two consecutive observations inside band by at least 0.2Â°C. The start is bounded by `[last in-band, first out-of-band]`; return by `[last out-of-band, first stable in-band]`; duration low = max(0, earliest return - latest start), high = latest return - earliest start, computed only across continuously covered spans. Initial out-of-band sample with no prior in-band gives unknown start; window end without return gives open episode and no finite high. `degree_minutes` bounds use observed rectangles only between adjacent usable samples <=10 minutes apart: low uses min adjacent exceedance, high max adjacent exceedance times interval minutes; add no unobserved gap area. Peak is the maximum **observed** value. An exposure Finding requires 15-minute lower duration and coverage >=0.8; otherwise `POSSIBLE` or `UNKNOWN`, never â€œno excursionâ€ from missing samples. In-band fully covered is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` scoped to window, not equipment health.

Start chain groups by accepted `correlation_id`, generator asset and outage window. Require ordered grid LOST â†’ START_REQUEST â†’ START_RESULT â†’ generator RUNNING/FAILED â†’ optional TRANSFER_RESULT â†’ bus POWERED/UNPOWERED â†’ compressor state â†’ probe response; tolerance is source-clock bound <=30 seconds for event ordering, with timeout from effective Foundation (default 5 minutes). A failed automatic start is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with request, explicit failed result or timeout plus complete generator state coverage through timeout, and no contradictory success; missing request/result/state is `UNKNOWN_STEP`. A transfer-position claim requires accepted transfer event; bus energization alone proves only bus state. Cooling failure cannot be assigned from temperature alone. Emit claim levels `OBSERVED`, `BOUNDED`, `POSSIBLE`, `UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY`; explanations list exact missing/excluded records and claim ceiling.

Episode key = SHA-256 of `(tenant,site,kind,subject room/generator asset,first accepted qualifying crossing/request event ID)`; retain anchor when late evidence adjusts boundaries, append `cold_episode_revision` and corresponding FindingRevision with transition `SUPPORT_CHANGED` or `BASIS_REFRESHED`. Two episodes split only after two stable in-band points (excursion) or a new start request (backup). If late evidence invalidates the anchor, append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` and create a new episode with `split_from` relation; never rewrite old. Separate excursion and start-response Findings can cite the same event but share one `overlap_group` for money. Use existing S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance and source classification (`backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-87`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

## 6. W2-04 consequence, reads and UI

`PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is a bounded thermal exposure indicator in `degC_min`, **not product quantity, spoilage or loss**. `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value` (currency/unit) and a product-specific reviewed `cold_exposure_rule` would be needed for a future valued risk scenario; W2 creates no default and still forbids an amount without an approved rule. `RECORDED_REPAIR` amount requires exact accepted work/cost record, currency, time and non-overlap; no such source exists in W2, so it is `UNPRICED`. All consequences pin FindingRevision, episode revision, accepted IDs, assumption revision IDs, model/formula version, source gate and overlap group. Missing/contradictory probe or policy, stale projection, mixed/unknown provenance, undeclared stock or cost â†’ `UNPRICED`/`WITHHELD` with null amount. Never sum backup and excursion exposure; no zero amount as stand-in for unknown. The existing LR current-money and S4-05 source gates still apply (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:1-30`; `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-69`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

All routes below use Site code, authenticated tenant scope, UTC `from,to` (end exclusive), optional UTC `known_at` (default current committed watermark), max 31-day window, and return `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. `watermark` is latest included ingestion disposition `decided_at` plus assessment revision ID; a selected historical cutoff cannot claim today's currentness. Lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, `limit` default 50 max 200 and `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared refusal envelope `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/source-gated disclosure, 404 Site/record not found or wrong type, 409 unresolved selected revision or recompute conflict, 422 malformed/window/range/unknown parameter, 503 ledger/projection unavailable; a valid missing claim returns 200 with `UNKNOWN`/null, never 404 or a fabricated zero. Existing evidence/Finding paths continue to serve exact links (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:157-161`; `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:102-189`).

Existing assumption publication endpoint handles writes. |

## 7. Slice proof fixtures and acceptance

### Writer guard and money trigger contract

Rule 8 in `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1`: add `cold_episode,cold_episode_revision,cold_episode_evidence` to `$assessTables` before `$assessInserts` is derived; add `cold_consequence_revision,cold_consequence_evidence,cold_consequence_work,cold_consequence_work_attempt` to `$consequenceTables`. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts episode and analytic/assessment rows; `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts Findings. `assessment_worker.py` owns one caller-supplied transaction spanning both adapters and job completion. `postgres_consequence_store.py` alone inserts consequence rows and append-only work attempts; migration triggers alone insert work. The consequence writer leases with an advisory transaction lock per work ID, appends one `SUCCEEDED` attempt atomically with consequence/links or a `FAILED` attempt with error code, and never updates/deletes work or attempts. Cross-writer checks protect all four tables. The ledger alone inserts event evidence, rejection and disposition in one transaction. Every accepted-evidence INSERT uses only the message constraint conflict target; event and START unique violations are handled by per-ordinal savepoint rollback and ordered reread (message, event, session) as in Â§2. No `DO UPDATE` permission is added.

Set `$ledgerConflictAllowed` to the double-quoted PowerShell regex `"(?i)\bON\s+CONFLICT\s+ON\s+CONSTRAINT\s+(?:batch_refusal_identity|ingestion_batch_external_unique|accepted_evidence_message_unique)\s+DO\s+NOTHING\b"`; retain its "three" target error text. Do not allow an event partial-index conflict target. `ledger_reads.py` uses `kind IN ('MESSAGE_CONFLICT','EVENT_ID_CONFLICT','SESSION_ID_CONFLICT')` in both receipt count locations (lines 232 and 376), retaining existing `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` objects and bytes. No migration touches old Receipt V2 rows.

Cold money work is triggered by appended cold FindingRevision or episode revision, relevant assumption publication or source-gate change; a migration trigger inserts append-only dirty `(site,window,domain='cold_chain')` work with unique dirty key and `DO NOTHING` duplicate suppression. `financials/recompute.py` runs the consequence worker; it selects unfinished work lacking a successful attempt, locks per work ID, selects the exact settled assessment/Finding basis, writes immutable consequence/evidence rows through `postgres_consequence_store.py`, and appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt in the same transaction. Failure appends a `FAILED` attempt with an error code, leaving work retryable; no work status UPDATE exists. `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` gains pass probes for each owner/table, message-target conflict and attempt append, and fail probes for runtime work INSERT, foreign writer, immutable UPDATE/DELETE, `DO UPDATE`, and event partial-index conflict target. Include double-collision probes: message+event must choose message; event+session with a free message must choose event. `configuration-persistence.ps1` counts probes and fails closed when an owner is absent. `overlap_group` is SHA-256 of canonical `(tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` prefixed `cold:`; excursion and backup consequences for that room/window share it.

### Response vocabulary

Every object includes `as_of: UTC timestamp|null` and `limitation: string|null`. Common `currentness.state` is exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`; `source_provenance` is `{classification: ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|AUTHORED|MIXED|UNKNOWN, source_ids: string[], evidence_ids: UUID[], basis_sha256: string|null}`; root `limitations` is `string[]`. `start_bounds,end_bounds,episode_bounds` are `{earliest: UTC timestamp|null,latest: UTC timestamp|null}`; `duration_bounds,degree_minute_bounds,quantity_bounds,amount_bounds` are `{low: decimal|null,high: decimal|null}`. `gaps` is `[{start:UTC timestamp,end:UTC timestamp,reason:MISSING_PROBE|SUSPECT|CLOCK_UNATTESTED|CONTRADICTORY|UNBOUND,expected_points:int,received_points:int}]`, with half-open bounds. `coverage` is a decimal in `[0,1]` plus `{known_points:int,expected_points:int}` on temperature/evidence cards.

| Field | Exact shape |
| --- | --- |
| `status` | `{state: OBSERVED_EXCURSION|NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level:OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of,limitation}` |
| `objective` | `{text:string,policy_low_deg_c:decimal|null,policy_high_deg_c:decimal|null,room_asset_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}` |

## 1. Migration ledger after 0023

All new IDs are UUID, times `timestamptz` UTC, quantities finite Postgres `numeric` (no floats), SHA fields lowercase 64-character hex. All new rows have `(tenant_id,site_id)` and composite same-Site FKs to parent/asset/evidence/revision; unique `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` wherever a composite FK targets a new table. Use append-only triggers on fact and revision tables, S1 effective/recorded validity for Foundation/bindings, and transactionally enforced uniqueness; never fabricate historical observations (`backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`; `backend/migrations/versions/0007_dispatch_assessment.py:38-125`; `backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-139`).

`ev_session_revision` holds a selected assessment snapshot, never a mutable session status. Existing `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `finding`, `finding_revision` retain exact basis/source lineage; EV `analytic_id=ev-session/1`. Later terminal/late records append revisions; invalidation uses `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED`, never deletion. One Finding per scoped session, no Finding for missing end (`backend/migrations/versions/0007_dispatch_assessment.py:38-125`; `backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-107`).

W2-00 enforces one accepted START per `(tenant,site,connector asset,session_id)` and returns `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`/`CONFLICT` with the first START evidence reference for a distinct second START. A conflicting END or ABORT with a distinct event ID remains accepted for the analytic `CONTRADICTORY` state. `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` covers reuse of one event ID with changed bytes; the four `EV_*` validation refusals are `REJECTED` with null conflict reference. Receipt V2 counts both new conflict codes under `conflicts`.

## 2. W2-05a Foundation, role set, readiness

Register immutable `ev-roles-1` in the site-type role registry; bind by effective Foundation and source mapping revisions (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). Topology is Site feed â†’ Site import meter â†’ EV charger â†’ EV connector; each connector has exactly one parent charger, each charger one feed, each asset ID distinct, no topology cycles or cross-Site links. Charger/connector serial labels are display metadata, never session IDs. Site import limit and charger/connector ratings are effective Foundation facts, not measured capacity. Connector enable schedule is a time-valid authored schedule in Foundation (`weekly local intervals`, Site timezone, explicit UTC offset resolution for DST); W2 uses it for configured service hours only, not an availability percentage. Reject overlapping intervals, nonexistent/ambiguous local instants and enabled connector without a declared charger/feed/meter. No stationary battery unless explicitly declared.

| Role / asset | v4 kind, unit, cardinality and gate |
| --- | --- |
| `ev_session_event` / EV_CONNECTOR | `EV_SESSION_START|END|ABORT`, one event channel per connector; session ID + connector ID required, `correlation_id=session_id`; required for outcome. |
| `ev_connector_fault` / EV_CONNECTOR | `EV_CONNECTOR_FAULT|CLEAR` event, one per connector; optional for completion, required for fault-caused interruption. Fault event's optional session ID must match same connector when attached. |
| `ev_connector_state` / EV_CONNECTOR | point `AVAILABLE|PLUGGED|AUTHORIZING|CHARGING|FAULTED|DISABLED`, `state`, one per connector, declared max 1-minute cadence; required for observed service-state context, never substitutes for terminal event. |
| `ev_connector_energy` / EV_CONNECTOR | nonnegative `INTERVAL_DELTA`, kWh, one meter channel per connector, 5-minute half-open slots; required for supported energy. W2-00 v4 already includes this kind with `conversion_kind='INTERVAL_DELTA'`, numeric kWh, positive interval, nonnegative value and explicit availability/quality. Its v4-only allowlist leaves v3 bytes/semantics unchanged. Mapping accepts this kind only on this role. |
| `ev_connector_power` / EV_CONNECTOR | nonnegative `INTERVAL_MEAN`, kW, one per connector, optional corroboration; never replaces energy meter. |
| `ev_site_import` / METER | nonnegative `INTERVAL_MEAN`, kW, ONE per Site feed, 5-minute aligned slots; required only for a bounded observed site-power comparison, not for session outcome/energy. |
| `ev_requested_power`,`ev_allocated_limit` / EV_CONNECTOR | nonnegative `INTERVAL_MEAN`, kW, optional independently reported controller channels; both plus synchronized site import/limit needed before a power-constraint explanation. Private vehicle request is never accepted demand evidence. |

Each role has registered source/device/signal, same-Site asset, exact unit and mapping, effective validity, cadence and clock bound. One physical signal cannot silently serve incompatible roles. `INTERVAL_DELTA` is an interval energy fact, never a cumulative counter. If a vendor supplies cumulative meter values, the labelled peer adapter converts only adjacent monotone readings with exact timestamps, meter reset/rollover declaration and error bound; reset or ambiguous jump yields no delta. Missing role, wrong unit/asset, overlapping bindings, ambiguous charger parent, invalid schedule, or cadence > configured ceiling refuse publication with typed configuration error; old Foundation revisions remain selectable. Readiness messages are fixed: `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`, `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND`, `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED`, `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. First six are configuration/capability, contact is S7 receipt state, commissioning needs an actual record. `READY_TO_REPORT` means configured, never commissioned/healthy (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`; `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:38-57`).

## 3. W2-05b versioned charger/session producer

Add `ev-charger/1` `ModelSpec`, `ev-publication/1`, `ev-observation/1` to the part-1 closed host registry, preserving all existing model identities (`host/execution_adapter.py:123-149`; `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:59-69`). Reject unknown pack/profile/parameter version before Draft. Frozen run identity includes Foundation/binding/source IDs, scenario SHA, pack/profile/observation versions, UTC window and seed. Stage seals only public v4 reports; registered Commit â†’ SQLite outbox â†’ authenticated Batch V2 â†’ S2 accepted evidence. No direct evidence/Finding seed, Lab private state, vehicle/customer identity or hidden demand in envelopes (`host/lab_execution.py:2154-2164,2428-2501`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:131,183`).

**Discrete model.** One-minute UTC step, exact decimal arithmetic; default fixture has two 60 kW connectors, 80 kW site import cap, both enabled [08:00,16:00) UTC on 2026-10-06, site non-EV base load 0 kW. State per connector: `UNPLUGGED|PLUGGED|AUTHORIZING|CHARGING|FAULTED`, session ID or null, authorization result, requested kW, allocated kW, meter accumulator kWh, fault latch. Private forcing: plug/unplug, authorization accept/deny, request schedule, fault/clear, grid interruption, publication dropout/latency. Initial unplugged, no session/fault, accumulator 0. At each minute: apply forcing ordered `(time,connector,kind)`; a plug assigns next deterministic scoped session ID; authorization takes 1 minute; acceptance emits START when CHARGING begins; denial produces no START and is a contextual observed state, not a failed session. Allocate shared available power `max(0,site_limit-base_load)` by connector ID ascending in round-robin 1 kW quanta among charging connectors up to `min(request,connector_limit,charger_limit)`; persist round-robin pointer so no connector monopolizes successive minutes. `E_next=E+allocated_kW/60`, exact kWh. A fault latches FAULTED, emits FAULT and ABORT for an active started session; explicit user unplug emits END (completed) only after active charge and no fault; grid interruption pauses allocation and connector state, it does not invent ABORT. Fault clear requires explicit forcing and emits CLEAR; a new plug creates a new session. Simultaneous END/ABORT or START twice for one ID is prohibited by simulator sanity guard. Fail run on negative energy, allocated sum above available/site/connector limits, energy residual, state with no valid session, duplicate conflicting forcing or non-monotone time.

**Public transform.** `ev-publication/1`: connector state at transitions and every minute; connector interval energy delta over aligned five-minute `[start,end)` slots (including zero while an active accepted session pauses), with session ID in the W2-00 v4 measurement payload; power mean per slot and site import mean per slot; session/fault events at observed transitions. Every v4 measurement contains `session_id,connector_id`; both are nonnull for connector energy and explicit null on cold/site channels. Connector ID must equal the bound EV_CONNECTOR asset; S2 refuses `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH`. `source_time=interval_end` for interval reports. Split a session boundary inside a slot into nonoverlapping subintervals. Round accumulator to 0.001 kWh half-even at both ends and difference rounded values, preserving additive deltas; meter bound 0.001 kWh per boundary. Event IDs derive from scenario SHA, channel and transition ordinal; retransmission preserves IDs/bytes. Observation gap drops a channel report, never changes the world; delayed report retains source time and message identity. Mapping `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` exactly allows `(charger-controller,session-event)`, `(connector-controller,fault-event)`, `(connector-controller,state)`, `(connector-meter,energy)`, `(connector-meter,power)`, `(site-meter,import)`, `(connector-controller,requested-power)`, `(connector-controller,allocated-limit)` with listed kinds/units/cadences (`simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:22-46,87-107`).

| Scenario `config/scenarios/ev-*.yaml` / seed 20517 | Complete UTC forcing `(time,kind,connector,value)` and observation change | Numeric public assertion, selected `[08:00,09:00)` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `complete` | `(08:00,PLUG,c1,session-1)`, `(08:00,AUTHORIZE,c1,ACCEPT)`, `(08:01,REQUEST_KW,c1,60)`, `(08:31,UNPLUG,c1,END)`; c2 stays unplugged. | START 08:01, END 08:31; 30 minutes Ã— 60/60 = 30.000 kWh complete, no Finding. Split intervals at 08:01 and 08:31. |
| `interrupted` | Same first three c1 rows; `(08:16,FAULT,c1,LATCH)`; c2 stays unplugged. | START 08:01, FAULT+ABORT 08:16; 15.000 kWh complete pre-abort, `OBSERVED_ABORT` and one fault-associated Finding. |
| `fault` | `(08:00,PLUG,c1,session-1)`, `(08:00,FAULT,c1,LATCH)`, `(08:05,CLEAR,c1,CLEAR)`; `(08:10,PLUG,c2,session-2)`, `(08:10,AUTHORIZE,c2,ACCEPT)`, `(08:11,REQUEST_KW,c2,60)`, `(08:21,UNPLUG,c2,END)`. | c1 no START/ABORT/Finding; CLEAR at 08:05 removes its fault latch and returns c1 to observed PLUGGED without starting a session; c2 10.000 kWh complete. Same-time ordering PLUG then FAULT suppresses authorization. |
| `missing-meter` | `complete` forcing; observation drop for slot `[08:10,08:15)`, `ENERGY_REPORT,c1,DROP`. | `COMPLETED`, 25.000 supported kWh, `energy_complete=false`, 300 unknown seconds, no priced total. |
| `late-record` | `complete` forcing; observation hold `(08:31,EV_SESSION_END,c1,RELEASE_AT_09:05)`. | First cutoff 09:00 `INCOMPLETE` and 30.000 kWh known partial; release preserves source time/ID and enqueues `ev_charging`, new revision `COMPLETED`, 30.000 kWh complete. |
| `contradictory` | `complete` world forcing; frozen observation fault emits an additional GOOD `EV_SESSION_END` for `session-1` at 08:30 with a distinct event ID, before genuine END 08:31. | Both individually valid public reports are accepted; `CONTRADICTORY`, null complete energy and amount, no Finding. Model still emits only its single genuine terminal, preserving the simulator safety guard. |

All scenarios use `ev-scenario/1`, frozen window/seed/profile and initial unplugged/no-session/no-fault/0 kWh state. Enabled schedule is [08:00,16:00) UTC; c1/c2 each max 60 kW and Site cap 80 kW, base load 0. The single charging connector receives 60 kW every active minute. `simulator/assetops_simulator/observation/transform.py` owns `ev-observation/1` hold/drop/fault variants; they alter public reports only. No mock 12/10/1/1, 120 kWh or $36 is injected (`frontend/mockups/v69/screens.js:433-450`). `Role.value_kind` includes `EVENT` and `INTERVAL_DELTA`; EVENT conversion is `IDENTITY` with outcome allowlist, delta conversion is `IDENTITY_KWH` with exact decimal and no counter interpretation. Lab persisted-state validator uses the EV connector state vocabulary of `ev-charger/1`, not the mini-grid generator lifecycle.

## 4. W2-06 session correlation, meter sum, Finding, currentness

Select only accepted, GOOD, clock-attested records with valid effective Foundation/binding and exact `(tenant,site,charger,connector,session_id)`; EVENT session ID comes from W2-00, energy identity from the v4 measurement extension above. Require one START, at most one terminal END or ABORT, ordered by source time with <=30-second declared cross-source clock error; a terminal without START remains orphan context, not a session. An END is COMPLETED only when same scoped ID, no competing ABORT, and no contradictory state; explicit ABORT proves OBSERVED_ABORT, while contemporaneous fault on the same connector/session is additionally required for a **fault-associated** Finding. Fault alone does not prove an abort. Missing terminal is INCOMPLETE regardless of apparently idle status; conflicting good terminal/start times or incompatible connector identity is CONTRADICTORY. `SUSPECT` and unbound reports are context/excluded with reasons, never settle outcomes. Repeat events are deduped by W2-00 and cross-tenant/session collisions cannot join.

For each session, intersect GOOD connector `INTERVAL_DELTA` reports with `[start,terminal)` and same session ID; accept only wholly contained, nonoverlapping half-open intervals with finite `0<=kWh<=connector_max_kW*duration_hours+2*meter_error_bound`, and matching exact source. Do **not** pro-rate a report spanning a boundary, choose between conflicting duplicates, substitute power integration or infer zero for an absent slot. `supported_kwh=sum(exact accepted deltas)`; `unknown_meter_seconds` is the union duration of uncovered active 5-minute slots, clipped to session bounds. A delivered-energy total is **complete** only if every active slot is covered (partial boundary slots must have a split report) and no contradiction; otherwise report known partial sum with `energy_complete=false` and unknown time, never call it total delivered. If no supported intervals, quantity null rather than zero. Session outcome counts use START denominator, separately completed/observed abort/incomplete/contradictory; orphan records are separate. No availability percentage or site constraint cause in W2. Synchronized site import below limit is context only; a power-constraint claim requires independent accepted requested and allocated-limit signals plus site import over the same slots, and W2 still labels competing causes unknown (`.agent/architect-W2-design.md:37-49`).

`ev-session/1` assessment basis SHA sorts accepted evidence IDs+content digests, excluded/context IDs+reasons, effective Foundation/binding/mapping IDs, model/rule versions, `[from,to)`, `known_at` and source classification. Session revision and its evidence/interval links append atomically with `assessment_revision` and any FindingRevision. A fault-abort Finding uses the stable key above, with exact fault/abort/START evidence and limitation â€œfault observed; component cause and unmet demand unknown.â€ A completed late END supersedes prior INCOMPLETE by new revision; a contradictory terminal withdraws previous current conclusion. Worker enqueue on **every** newly accepted relevant EV report, including late source time; until latest applicable job settles, previous projection reads `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`, with historical exact revision separately selectable. For current status, latest needed connector state <=2 minutes old at read time and terminal/event sources within declared cadence; old closed sessions may remain historical conclusions. Current service-window coverage uses one-minute status slots and explicit `known_seconds/unknown_seconds`; no available-time inference over gaps >2 minutes. Source gate follows S4-05 exact revision provenance; generated and authored evidence are labelled and mixed/unknown blocks a current positive (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15,4165-4188`).

## 5. W2-07 tariff scenario and claim gate

`ev_gross_tariff_per_kwh` is a Site time-valid, recorded revision in `currency/kWh`, with authored basis/source; no default and no billing/collection identity. A window may price only supported energy in completed or observed-abort sessions, each with complete meter coverage and settled current assessment; no session interval is counted twice. For every included meter interval, select exactly one tariff revision whose **valid** range covers it and whose **recorded** range is visible at `known_at`; W2 v1 requires the same revision and currency across the entire priced window, otherwise `UNPRICED/PRICE_GAP_OR_CHANGE` (no averaging). Formula `amount = Î£ exact interval kWh Ã— tariff value`, unrounded in storage; display rounding only. `quantity_kwh` and exact session revision/accepted meter evidence/tariff revision IDs are pinned. No lost-sales estimate: private requested power, incomplete sessions and fault downtime do not measure counterfactual demand. An aborted session can contribute **delivered** pre-abort energy, never lost energy. W1 LR current-money gate and S4-05 coherent source gate apply: pending/late/revised assessment, conflicting meter, historical cutoff, mixed/unknown provenance, unsupported tariff, or missing price â†’ `WITHHELD` or `UNPRICED`, null amount (not zero). Historical amount may appear labelled historical only under its exact old basis, never as today's current money. `overlap_group='ev:'+sha256(canonical(tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,tariff_revision_id))` prevents adding the same meter intervals through multiple Findings; Site scenario is one window revision, not per-Finding additive totals (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:38-102`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

## 6. Selected reads and refusals

All routes are tenant-authenticated Site-code reads. Except Foundation/replay, require UTC half-open `from,to`, max 31 days, optional UTC `known_at` (default latest committed watermark); a historical cutoff is labelled historical. Common response `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. Watermark pins latest included S2 decision time + assessment revision; lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, default limit 50/max 200, `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared error `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/Lab gate/source-gated detail, 404 missing/wrong Site type or record, 409 unresolved selected revision or conflicting current recompute, 422 bad parameter/window/selector, 503 ledger/projection unavailable. Valid evidence gaps return 200 with UNKNOWN/null and reasons. No endpoint returns private forcing/oracle, customer/vehicle or a fabricated zero (`.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:83-107`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:54-59,157-161`).

| Route / slice | Additional fields and selection |
| --- | --- |
| `GET /api/sites/{code}/ev/foundation?at=` / 05a | Effective `foundation_revision_id,valid_from,valid_to,feed:{asset_id,limit_kw},chargers[{asset_id,max_kw,connectors:[{asset_id,max_kw,enabled_schedule}]}],topology:{nodes,edges},bindings[{role,asset_id,source_id,unit,cadence,valid_from,valid_to}],readiness:{state,messages},commissioning:{state,evidence_ids},contact:{state,as_of}`; `at` defaults now. |

Existing assumption publication route accepts new key with same authorization/idempotency rules. |

## 8. Slice fixtures, replay and vendor peer

### Writer, trigger and transaction contract

The 0022 generalization is mandatory before 0025. For `ev_charging`, `analytic_run.cohort_status='NOT_APPLICABLE'`, `cohort=[]`, `cohort_sha256=sha256(canonical([]))`; the complete Finding/Revision NOT NULL mapping and `'mini_grid'` keyed CHECKs in the cold design apply. EV FindingRevision `episode_start=START source_time`, `episode_end=ABORT source_time`; its three dispatch minute columns are null. `support_sha256` hashes sorted exact START/FAULT/ABORT evidence ID+SHA pairs, `cited_evidence_sha256` hashes the exact cited accepted set, and `input_evidence_sha256=analytic_run.input_sha256`. No fault-abort Finding is created before all three accepted facts exist.

Rule 8 `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1`: add `ev_session,ev_session_revision,ev_session_evidence,ev_meter_interval` to `$assessTables` before `$assessInserts` is derived; add `ev_consequence_revision,ev_consequence_session,ev_consequence_evidence` to `$consequenceTables`, then add `ev_consequence_work,ev_consequence_work_attempt` to the same `$consequenceTables` array. `postgres_assessment_store.py` exclusively inserts the four session tables, `postgres_finding_store.py` exclusively inserts Findings, and `postgres_consequence_store.py` exclusively inserts the three EV consequence tables and appends work attempts. Migration triggers alone insert work; the consequence writer appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt atomically with consequence/links or a `FAILED` attempt with error code. Work and attempts are append-only; runtime work INSERT, UPDATE and DELETE are rejected. Cross-writer checks cover `$consequenceTables`. `assessment_worker.py` owns one caller-supplied transaction containing analytic run, assessment, session revision/links, FindingRevision/links and job completion. `financials/recompute.py` owns a separate transaction containing consequence/links and successful work-attempt append. No SQL writer enters HTTP/domain modules and no `DO UPDATE` allowlist entry is added. The 0020 message-only conflict target, per-ordinal savepoint/reread precedence and Receipt V2 classification are inherited unchanged. `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` adds permitted owner INSERT and work-attempt append, rejects runtime work INSERT, immutable work/attempt UPDATE or DELETE, foreign owner, immutable UPDATE/DELETE, unlisted `ON CONFLICT` and dynamic SQL; it includes pass/fail probes for both multi-adapter transaction paths. The guard fails when a named module/table is missing.

`0026` adds append-only `ev_consequence_work` (`id uuid PK`, tenant/site, window_start/end, dirty_basis_sha256, created_at; UNIQUE `(site_id,window_start,window_end,dirty_basis_sha256)`) and `ev_consequence_work_attempt` (`id uuid PK`, tenant/site, work_id scoped FK, attempt_no int>0, outcome `SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, error_code nullable, started_at, finished_at; UNIQUE `(work_id,attempt_no)`, partial unique successful attempt per work). Migration triggers insert dirty work on appended EV session/assessment revision, accepted late EV meter/event, tariff publication, and source-gate revision. Duplicate dirtiness uses `DO NOTHING` on the unique key inside the trigger, not a runtime writer. `postgres_consequence_store.py` selects unfinished work lacking a successful attempt, takes an advisory transaction lock per work ID, writes a Site window revision and links, and appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt atomically; failure appends `FAILED` and remains retryable. `ev_consequence_revision.revision_no` is `1+max(revision_no)` for the locked `(tenant,site,window_start,window_end)` lineage, guarded by unique `(site_id,window_start,window_end,revision_no)` and retried on collision. `overlap_group='ev:'+sha256(canonical(tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,tariff_revision_id))`, using null tariff ID text `NO_TARIFF` for unpriced revisions.

### Response vocabulary

Use the cold design's common `currentness.state`, `source_provenance`, `limitations`, UUID/time/decimal JSON types and exact envelope. EV `start_bounds,end_bounds` are `{earliest:UTC timestamp|null,latest:UTC timestamp|null}`. Session `reason_codes` is an array drawn only from `MISSING_START|MISSING_TERMINAL|CONFLICTING_TERMINAL|CONFLICTING_START|CONNECTOR_MISMATCH|METER_GAP|METER_OVERLAP|METER_INVALID|CLOCK_UNATTESTED|SOURCE_UNVERIFIED|PENDING_RECOMPUTE`. `links` is `{self:string|null,evidence:string|null,analysis:string|null,finding:string|null,financials:string|null}`; null means no supported destination. Foundation `readiness.state=READY_TO_REPORT|CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE`, `commissioning.state=CONFIRMED|UNCONFIRMED`, `contact.state=RECENT|STALE|NEVER`; `messages` uses exactly the codes in Â§2. Capability `state=SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY` and `claim=SESSION_OUTCOME|DELIVERED_ENERGY|FAULT_ASSOCIATION|SERVICE_STATE|SITE_POWER_CONTEXT|POWER_CONSTRAINT|GROSS_TARIFF_SCENARIO`; `required_roles,missing_roles:string[]`, `coverage={known_seconds:int,expected_seconds:int,ratio:decimal}`. `site_power.coverage` has that same shape; `claim_limit` is a string drawn from `CONTEXT_ONLY|REQUEST_AND_LIMIT_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN`.

| Named EV field | Exact shape |
| --- | --- |
| `status` | `{state:OBSERVED_SESSIONS|NO_SESSIONS_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level:OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of:UTC timestamp|null,limitation:string|null}` |
| `objective` | `{text:string,configured_service_window:{from:UTC timestamp,to:UTC timestamp},site_import_limit_kw:decimal|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `findings` | `{count:int,items:[{finding_id:UUID,revision_id:UUID,title:string,claim_level:string,link:string}],as_of,limitation}` |
| `needs_attention` | `[{code:METER_GAP|INCOMPLETE_SESSION|FAULT_ABORT|SOURCE_STALE|RECOMPUTE_PENDING|TARIFF_MISSING,text:string,link:string|null,as_of,limitation}]` |
| `consequence_summary` | `{state:ESTIMATED|UNPRICED|WITHHELD,quantity_kwh:decimal|null,amount:decimal|null,currency:string|null,overlap_group:string|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `source_provenance,limitations` | Same exact common shape as cold; every EV card includes its own `as_of` and `limitation`. |

Undeclared reset/rollover refuses `EV_COUNTER_RESET`, absent/ambiguous timestamp `EV_VENDOR_TIME_INVALID`, unknown connector `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH`, unsupported unit `EV_DELTA_UNIT_INVALID`. ## Domain seams and claim rules

Reuse tenant/Site identity, immutable time-valid Foundation and binding revisions, source registrations, typed `EvidenceQuery`, S2 AcceptedEvidence/receipt, selected half-open window and knowledge cutoff, revisioned Finding/financial consequence, S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance, W1 Site shell and separate Analysis destination (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:67-84`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:22-54,173-183`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:13-19,4165-4188`). Keep `COLDCHAIN` and `EV_CHARGING` as explicit site types; bind each physical source signal to asset, semantic role, unit, cadence, valid period and mapping/Foundation revision. A signal cannot fill two incompatible roles by label alone. Historical revisions remain inspectable; absent/stale/contradictory roles produce claim-specific Unknown. No status is inferred from gateway contact alone.

| Contract | Cold-chain | EV charging |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Foundation/roles | Chamber/probe and policy low/high Â°C required for excursion; compressor run/power, grid import/status, generator request/state, backup bus or inverter state required **per causal step**. Door, defrost, ambient, load and transfer position optional context whose absence blocks those explanations. Policy and calibration versioned; readings in UTC with Site-local display. | Connector identity and enabled schedule, session ID/start/end/outcome, interval meter kWh, connector status, site import kW and effective site/charger limits. Request/current limit and vehicle demand optional for power-constraint diagnosis; tariff/billing independent. Require declared status cadence and connector schedule for availability, which W2 does not promise. |
| Evidence/assessment | Validated Â°C samples and policy yield crossing interval `[last in-band, first out-of-band]` and return interval; duration/Â°CÂ·min lower/upper bounds, coverage, peak **observed** value. Missing probe segment cannot be bridged. Backup chain matches ordered accepted events within declared timestamp tolerance, reports each missing step and never infers starter-part fault from timing. Distinguish temperature excursion, observed failed automatic start, and unknown cause. | Correlate records by scoped charger/connector/session ID; duplicate/conflicting IDs quarantine. Start + end + accepted metered intervals establish delivered energy; observed fault/stop establishes abort; absent end remains incomplete. Summaries keep complete/aborted/incomplete denominators and known/unknown time. Below-limit site import is context, not proof that the site supply did not constrain a session without synchronized request/limit/voltage evidence. |
| Finding/currentness | Separate excursion and start-response Finding identities/revisions, exact evidence IDs, configuration and model versions; both may cite the same event but no duplicated money. Late evidence queues recomputation and visibly marks previous revision stale until rebuilt. | Session-interruption Finding cites exact fault/stop/meter evidence; incomplete sessions are evidence gaps, not failures. Late end/status can supersede through a new revision; missing data never silently becomes normal. |
| Money | Excursion â†’ unpriced **potential product exposure** unless stock identity, quantity/value and a reviewed product-specific exposure-response rule are present; temperature alone never proves spoilage. Recorded call-out/repair expense needs an actual work/cost record. No summed loss across overlapping backup and excursion Findings. | Metered supported energy Ã— versioned tariff gives gross *scenario* estimate, not collections, margin or lost revenue. Lost revenue requires credible counterfactual demand, price and service baseline; W2 keeps it unpriced. Warranty/repair scenarios wait for asset/work contracts. |
| Readiness/SLD | Reuse S1 configuration readiness and S7 source health separately. Draw declared grid/PV/battery/control bus/generator/compressor/probe links; label inferred electrical-to-cooling relationship as configuration, not live flow. | Draw effective site feed/meter/charger/connector links and configured limits; no stationary battery unless Foundation declares one. SLD monitoring markers derive from bindings + source health, never drawing alone. |

Both analytics consume only normalized AcceptedEvidence with quality, source/measurement/receive times, role and effective configuration. Output stores input digest (accepted IDs/content, mapping/Foundation, window and model version), bounded quantities, reasons and projection watermark. Clock skew, low cadence, gaps and contradiction can independently lower capability. Current-money reads retain LR and S4-05 source gates; `Generated test data` and `Prepared example data` labels are adjacent to material claim limits, mixed/unknown suppress current amounts (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:4165-4188`). W1 plain default with chart/timeline and contextual detail applies from each slice; no raw codes/IDs or simulator terms in the default reading (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:13-15`).

## Simulator-to-evidence contract

1. Add **versioned cold pack** to `simulator/assetops_simulator/packs/`: thermal stock (chamber/ambient heat exchange and load), door-opening heat pulse, compressor on/off and cooling capacity, control power versus compressor power, grid-loss/backup transfer and generator start/failure. Compose with existing electrical mini-grid machinery only at explicit power ports; no event timeline written directly into reports. Declare discrete states/transitions and initialization; step alignment and conservation/temperature sanity guards are executable. Private world has disturbance cause, target trajectory and oracle; public device transform sees only probe, meter, controller and manual-log observations (`simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/model.py:1-110`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/packs/mini_grid.py:1-100`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/publish.py:1-45`).
2. Add **versioned EV pack**: charger/connector availability, requests, negotiated limit, site shared-power cap, session start/energy/end/fault state, meter energy integration. A vehicle-request schedule is private forcing, not accepted demand evidence. Device transform publishes charger events/status, interval meter and site meter separately. Simulated missing end/status is a publication-path loss, not a fabricated `FAILED` event.
3. Extend `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` and registered Lab basis with exact pack/model version, Foundation values, role/source mapping, report cadence, observation bias/gaps, gateway publication schedule and frozen scenario digest; reject unsupported states before Draft. Add `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` and `ev-*.yaml` plus profile versions. Stage seals public envelopes; registered Commit binds run/batch before ordinary gateway release. Continue through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2, S2 receipt and AcceptedEvidence; no direct Finding or evidence seed and no private manifest in product bytes (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:67-75`; `host/gateway_publication.py:1-25`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:131,183`).
4. One deterministic replay manifest per case freezes Site/Foundation/binding IDs, scenario/profile/model/observation versions, UTC window, random seed if used, expected public batch/envelope digests, receipt dispositions, accepted IDs and bounded assessment assertions. Cases per domain: **positive** excursion/start failure and observed EV abort; **negative** in-policy/normal start and completed sessions; **missing** probe/transfer and end/status; **late** delayed accepted reports forcing new revision, with prior read stale. Include contradictory clock/role mapping refusal. Re-run same bytes for idempotent receipts and compare model-independent public physical-adapter peer after typed normalization. A labelled vendor-format fixture is a contract peer, not attested live source; actual physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` remain pilot gates (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-demo-data-plan-proposal.md:3-17`; `frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:79-86`).

# W1 structural fidelity: Architect contract and rework plan (2026-10-17)

Decision: `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. The owner rejected the combined W1 walkthrough; the verbatim verdict is `.agent/owner-W1-walkthrough-verdict-2026-10-17.md`. This is a layout correction, not a data or capability promotion. Scope is the 26 Site screens in the W1 walkthrough and W1 registry list; the registry also labels `lab` W1, but it is not one of the 26 requested screens and needs its own Lab comparison before a later owner session. The registry and its checker stay unchanged.

## Binding layout contract for W1 rework and W2+ UI

For every named screen, render the v69 panel **set, order, titles, relative prominence and size, grid, and visual style** from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, `mockups.css`, `screen-manifest.json`, and `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json`. Use the mock's page heading, compact metric, panel, two-column, timeline, badge, table and gap patterns; port missing tokens/patterns into `frontend/src/ui/tokens.css` and `primitives.css` or shared UI components. Existing type/colour tokens that already match should be reused. Compare the rendered mock at both widths; the JSON titles alone do not define spatial structure. The mock's illustrative values never become product values.

The panel title map below is exhaustive. Default title is exactly `v69-rendered-panels.json`. Approved plain-language changes are: `foundation:2` **Identity and operating obligations** for `Identity & operating obligations`; `dispatch:2` **Selected block Â· {actual selected interval}** for the mock's illustrative `14:20â€“14:35`; `readiness:2` **Gateway status Â· {actual gateway name or â€œNo gateway selectedâ€}** for `GW-MG-01`; `finding-evidence:1` **Evidence bundle Â· {actual Finding name or â€œSelected Findingâ€}** for `F-104`; `sld:1` **{actual site name} Â· {configured topology type, if stated}** for `Kobo Mini-grid Â· AC-coupled mini-grid`. Dynamic titles must use the selected read, never mock fixtures. An unknown dynamic value is labelled unknown in the same panel. No other title substitutions are approved here.

Each mock panel retains its slot when a selected read cannot support its content. Put the precise honest gap **inside that panel** (unavailable, refused, pending, stale, withdrawn, not assessed, absent, or proved zero as appropriate); never synthesize a value, omit the panel, or fill it with a different period's value. A page-level refusal or no-version state may state the refusal before the panel grid, but still renders the panel skeletons with in-panel gaps where the route and screen are valid. Place current app-only content within its owning mock panel or the named contextual Analysis/Evidence/History destination below. Remove the W1-UX leading answer cards and duplicate old panels after merging their supported answer, visible state/scope/as-of, and next action into the matching mock panel. No new leading card. Keep material limits visible beside the claim.

`D-2026-10-16-w1-ux` and `-w1-ux-disclosure` still govern contextual Analysis tabs, narrow exact-reference disclosures, plain words and honest claims. At 390 px the same Analysis destination stays accessible. A closed disclosure cannot hold substantive method or hide a material limit. `D-2026-10-17-w1-rail` and `-w1-ux-06` continue to govern navigation and the configuration/contact/condition distinction.

.agent/W2-technical-design-ev.md:39:**Discrete model.** One-minute UTC step, exact decimal arithmetic; default fixture has two 60 kW connectors, 80 kW site import cap, both enabled [08:00,16:00) UTC on 2026-10-06, site non-EV base load 0 kW. State per connector: `UNPLUGGED|PLUGGED|AUTHORIZING|CHARGING|FAULTED`, session ID or null, authorization result, requested kW, allocated kW, meter accumulator kWh, fault latch. Private forcing: plug/unplug, authorization accept/deny, request schedule, fault/clear, grid interruption, publication dropout/latency. Initial unplugged, no session/fault, accumulator 0. At each minute: apply forcing ordered `(time,connector,kind)`; a plug assigns next deterministic scoped session ID; authorization takes 1 minute; acceptance emits START when CHARGING begins; denial produces no START and is a contextual observed state, not a failed session. Allocate shared available power `max(0,site_limit-base_load)` by connector ID ascending in round-robin 1 kW quanta among charging connectors up to `min(request,connector_limit,charger_limit)`; persist round-robin pointer so no connector monopolizes successive minutes. `E_next=E+allocated_kW/60`, exact kWh. A fault latches FAULTED, emits FAULT and ABORT for an active started session; explicit user unplug emits END (completed) only after active charge and no fault; grid interruption pauses allocation and connector state, it does not invent ABORT. Fault clear requires explicit forcing and emits CLEAR; a new plug creates a new session. Simultaneous END/ABORT or START twice for one ID is prohibited by simulator sanity guard. Fail run on negative energy, allocated sum above available/site/connector limits, energy residual, state with no valid session, duplicate conflicting forcing or non-monotone time.
.agent/W2-technical-design-ev.md:41:**Public transform.** `ev-publication/1`: connector state at transitions and every minute; connector interval energy delta over aligned five-minute `[start,end)` slots (including zero while an active accepted session pauses), with session ID in the W2-00 v4 measurement payload; power mean per slot and site import mean per slot; session/fault events at observed transitions. Every v4 measurement contains `session_id,connector_id`; both are nonnull for connector energy and explicit null on cold/site channels. Connector ID must equal the bound EV_CONNECTOR asset; S2 refuses `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH`. `source_time=interval_end` for interval reports. Observation gap drops a channel report, never changes the world; delayed report retains source time and message identity. .agent/W2-technical-design-ev.md:54:## 4. W2-06 session correlation, meter sum, Finding, currentness
.agent/W2-technical-design-ev.md:56:Select only accepted, GOOD, clock-attested records with valid effective Foundation/binding and exact `(tenant,site,charger,connector,session_id)`; EVENT session ID comes from W2-00, energy identity from the v4 measurement extension above. Require one START, at most one terminal END or ABORT, ordered by source time with <=30-second declared cross-source clock error; a terminal without START remains orphan context, not a session. An END is COMPLETED only when same scoped ID, no competing ABORT, and no contradictory state; explicit ABORT proves OBSERVED_ABORT, while contemporaneous fault on the same connector/session is additionally required for a **fault-associated** Finding. Fault alone does not prove an abort. Missing terminal is INCOMPLETE regardless of apparently idle status; conflicting good terminal/start times or incompatible connector identity is CONTRADICTORY. `SUSPECT` and unbound reports are context/excluded with reasons, never settle outcomes. Repeat events are deduped by W2-00 and cross-tenant/session collisions cannot join.
.agent/W2-technical-design-ev.md:58:For each session, intersect GOOD connector `INTERVAL_DELTA` reports with `[start,terminal)` and same session ID; accept only wholly contained, nonoverlapping half-open intervals with finite `0<=kWh<=connector_max_kW*duration_hours+2*meter_error_bound`, and matching exact source. Do **not** pro-rate a report spanning a boundary, choose between conflicting duplicates, substitute power integration or infer zero for an absent slot. `supported_kwh=sum(exact accepted deltas)`; `unknown_meter_seconds` is the union duration of uncovered active 5-minute slots, clipped to session bounds. A delivered-energy total is **complete** only if every active slot is covered (partial boundary slots must have a split report) and no contradiction; otherwise report known partial sum with `energy_complete=false` and unknown time, never call it total delivered. If no supported intervals, quantity null rather than zero. Session outcome counts use START denominator, separately completed/observed abort/incomplete/contradictory; orphan records are separate. No availability percentage or site constraint cause in W2. Synchronized site import below limit is context only; a power-constraint claim requires independent accepted requested and allocated-limit signals plus site import over the same slots, and W2 still labels competing causes unknown (`.agent/architect-W2-design.md:37-49`).
