.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:5:## 1. W2-00: ordinary source-to-evidence format
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:17:Every v4 top-level object keeps the v3 exact keys; `message` keeps `message_id,sequence_epoch,sequence`, `source` and `mapping` keep v3 keys, `timing` keeps `source_time,published_at,interval_start,interval_end`. Events require null interval bounds; their source time is the observed transition, not a duration. For cold event `correlation_id` is the controller outage/attempt ID; a door pair shares its door-cycle ID. For EV, `correlation_id=session_id` on session events and optional registered fault episode ID on fault/clear. `event_id` is stable under retransmission and unique within `(site,source,record_kind)`; message ID remains transport identity. Canonical event payload contains explicit nulls for inapplicable fields. No arbitrary JSON detail, free text, Lab run ID, oracle, private cause, or stock identity. Event IDs generated from frozen scenario + public device channel + transition ordinal in Lab; physical adapters preserve vendor stable event ID or derive a documented hash from raw immutable event key. Cross-source event correlation is a claim input, never an acceptance dedup key.
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:31:| `0020_w2_source_event` (W2-00) | `accepted_evidence`: nullable `record_kind text`, `event_id text`, `correlation_id text`, `session_id text`, `connector_id text`, `actor text`, `outcome text`; relax `measurement`, `conversion_kind`, `original_unit`, `normalized_unit` to nullable; keep `quality` NOT NULL. MEASUREMENT requires the old nonnull shape, all event-only columns null, and paired session/connector IDs only for `INTERVAL_DELTA`; `conversion_kind='INTERVAL_DELTA'` requires `kWh`, positive interval, nonnegative value, paired EV IDs. .agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:49:## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:68:Foundation topology uses explicit `COLD_ROOM`→`TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`→room, bus→compressor, generator→transfer→bus relations; every referenced asset must exist, one chamber probe cannot be primary for two rooms, and cycles/duplicate edges are refused. Policy band belongs to a room and is effective for the selected window; changing it creates a Foundation revision and binding set selection, never an in-place update. Validate finite exact positive thermal parameters, `0<=hysteresis<high-low`, probe error `>=0`, timeout `>0`, and cadence 5 minutes for the first Lab profile. Publication readiness must say **“Add a chamber temperature probe and bind its °C reading”**, **“Set a lower and upper temperature policy for this room”**, **“Bind the generator start request and result”**, or **“Transfer position is not reported; switch state is unknown”** as applicable. Configuration readiness, commissioning evidence and gateway contact remain separate (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_service.py:267-317,674-715`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:32-53`).
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:70:## 4. W2-02 causal producer and publication
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:72:Add `cold-thermal/1` `ModelSpec` and `cold-publication/1`; resolve frozen profile identity in `host/execution_adapter.py:113-140`, `host/lab_execution.py:1978-2015` and `simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/execute.py:90-105` through a closed registry, with fuel/mini-grid identities preserved. Reject unknown pack/profile/parameter version before Draft. The kernel receives typed model laws and power ports, not a cold-specific branch. Lab freezes Foundation/binding IDs, scenario SHA, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity; Stage seals public v4 envelopes, registered Commit links publication, SQLite outbox sends authenticated Batch V2, and S2 alone creates accepted evidence (`host/lab_execution.py:2154-2164,2428-2501`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:55-66`).
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:74:**Time and state.** Fixed 1-minute UTC step; all updates use exact rationals/decimal text and a defined end-of-step publication order. State: chamber air temperature `T` degC, controller demand, compressor `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, grid/backup bus energized flags, generator `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, transfer position `GRID|BACKUP|OPEN`, door `OPEN|CLOSED`, probe `OK|FAILED`. Private forcing: ambient `Ta`, door schedule, grid loss, generator failure, probe failure; private truth never serializes into a product envelope. Initial `T=3°C`, `Ta=20°C`, door closed, compressor off, grid and bus on, generator stopped, transfer grid, probe OK. Per minute, `heat_kWh = (heat_leak_kW_per_C*(Ta-T) + door_open*door_heat_kWh*60)*1/60`; `cool_kWh = compressor_running_and_bus_powered*cooling_kW*1/60`; `T_next=T+(heat_kWh-cool_kWh)/thermal_capacity_kWh_per_C`. Defaults: thermal capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min` (stored as `1.2 kW` equivalent while open), cooling `3 kW`, compressor start delay 1 minute, generator start delay 2 minutes, transfer delay 1 minute, start timeout 5 minutes, control on `T>=4°C`, off `T<=2°C`, policy `[2,4]°C`, hysteresis 0.2°C, probe bound ±0.1°C. Clamp no state: fail run if `T` outside `[-40,60]°C`, energy residual nonzero, negative capacity/power, or impossible transition. The standalone cold pack owns its generator and bus power port; W2-02 does not compose with mini-grid.
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:91:The closed cold mapping uses `(device_id,signal_id)`: `(chamber-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(ambient-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(grid-meter,state)` state point, `(generator-controller,state)` state point, `(compressor-controller,state)` state point, `(backup-bus-meter,state)` state point, `(compressor-meter,power)` kW interval mean, `(door-sensor,event)` door events, `(generator-controller,start-event)` request/result events, `(transfer-controller,event)` transfer result, `(grid-meter,event)` loss/restored events, `(operator-log,temperature-event)` manual log. Each tuple is a separate versioned mapping entry with only its listed kinds, values and cadence. W2-00 also reserves EV entries `(charger-controller,session-event)` for session start/end/abort and `(connector-controller,fault-event)` for fault/clear, with connector/session identity rules above; W2-05b supplies the EV pack and its own frozen mapping version. The same signal ID on a different device is not an implicit alias (`simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:22-46,87-107`).

## 6. W2-04 consequence, reads and UI

`PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is a bounded thermal exposure indicator in `degC_min`, **not product quantity, spoilage or loss**. `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value` (currency/unit) and a product-specific reviewed `cold_exposure_rule` would be needed for a future valued risk scenario; W2 creates no default and still forbids an amount without an approved rule. `RECORDED_REPAIR` amount requires exact accepted work/cost record, currency, time and non-overlap; no such source exists in W2, so it is `UNPRICED`. All consequences pin FindingRevision, episode revision, accepted IDs, assumption revision IDs, model/formula version, source gate and overlap group. Missing/contradictory probe or policy, stale projection, mixed/unknown provenance, undeclared stock or cost â†’ `UNPRICED`/`WITHHELD` with null amount. Never sum backup and excursion exposure; no zero amount as stand-in for unknown. The existing LR current-money and S4-05 source gates still apply (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:1-30`; `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-69`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

All routes below use Site code, authenticated tenant scope, UTC `from,to` (end exclusive), optional UTC `known_at` (default current committed watermark), max 31-day window, and return `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. `watermark` is latest included ingestion disposition `decided_at` plus assessment revision ID; a selected historical cutoff cannot claim today's currentness. Lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, `limit` default 50 max 200 and `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared refusal envelope `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/source-gated disclosure, 404 Site/record not found or wrong type, 409 unresolved selected revision or recompute conflict, 422 malformed/window/range/unknown parameter, 503 ledger/projection unavailable; a valid missing claim returns 200 with `UNKNOWN`/null, never 404 or a fabricated zero. Existing evidence/Finding paths continue to serve exact links (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:157-161`; `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:102-189`).

## 7. Slice proof fixtures and acceptance

### Writer guard and money trigger contract

Rule 8 in `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1`: add `cold_episode,cold_episode_revision,cold_episode_evidence` to `$assessTables` before `$assessInserts` is derived; add `cold_consequence_revision,cold_consequence_evidence,cold_consequence_work,cold_consequence_work_attempt` to `$consequenceTables`. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts episode and analytic/assessment rows; `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts Findings. `assessment_worker.py` owns one caller-supplied transaction spanning both adapters and job completion. `postgres_consequence_store.py` alone inserts consequence rows and append-only work attempts; migration triggers alone insert work. The consequence writer leases with an advisory transaction lock per work ID, appends one `SUCCEEDED` attempt atomically with consequence/links or a `FAILED` attempt with error code, and never updates/deletes work or attempts. Cross-writer checks protect all four tables. The ledger alone inserts event evidence, rejection and disposition in one transaction. Every accepted-evidence INSERT uses only the message constraint conflict target; event and START unique violations are handled by per-ordinal savepoint rollback and ordered reread (message, event, session) as in Â§2. No `DO UPDATE` permission is added.

Set `$ledgerConflictAllowed` to the double-quoted PowerShell regex `"(?i)\bON\s+CONFLICT\s+ON\s+CONSTRAINT\s+(?:batch_refusal_identity|ingestion_batch_external_unique|accepted_evidence_message_unique)\s+DO\s+NOTHING\b"`; retain its "three" target error text. Do not allow an event partial-index conflict target. `ledger_reads.py` uses `kind IN ('MESSAGE_CONFLICT','EVENT_ID_CONFLICT','SESSION_ID_CONFLICT')` in both receipt count locations (lines 232 and 376), retaining existing `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` objects and bytes. No migration touches old Receipt V2 rows.

Cold money work is triggered by appended cold FindingRevision or episode revision, relevant assumption publication or source-gate change; a migration trigger inserts append-only dirty `(site,window,domain='cold_chain')` work with unique dirty key and `DO NOTHING` duplicate suppression. `financials/recompute.py` runs the consequence worker; it selects unfinished work lacking a successful attempt, locks per work ID, selects the exact settled assessment/Finding basis, writes immutable consequence/evidence rows through `postgres_consequence_store.py`, and appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt in the same transaction. Failure appends a `FAILED` attempt with an error code, leaving work retryable; no work status UPDATE exists. `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` gains pass probes for each owner/table, message-target conflict and attempt append, and fail probes for runtime work INSERT, foreign writer, immutable UPDATE/DELETE, `DO UPDATE`, and event partial-index conflict target. Include double-collision probes: message+event must choose message; event+session with a free message must choose event. `configuration-persistence.ps1` counts probes and fails closed when an owner is absent. `overlap_group` is SHA-256 of canonical `(tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` prefixed `cold:`; excursion and backup consequences for that room/window share it.

### Response vocabulary

Every object includes `as_of: UTC timestamp|null` and `limitation: string|null`. Common `currentness.state` is exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`; `source_provenance` is `{classification: ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|AUTHORED|MIXED|UNKNOWN, source_ids: string[], evidence_ids: UUID[], basis_sha256: string|null}`; root `limitations` is `string[]`. `start_bounds,end_bounds,episode_bounds` are `{earliest: UTC timestamp|null,latest: UTC timestamp|null}`; `duration_bounds,degree_minute_bounds,quantity_bounds,amount_bounds` are `{low: decimal|null,high: decimal|null}`. `gaps` is `[{start:UTC timestamp,end:UTC timestamp,reason:MISSING_PROBE|SUSPECT|CLOCK_UNATTESTED|CONTRADICTORY|UNBOUND,expected_points:int,received_points:int}]`, with half-open bounds. `coverage` is a decimal in `[0,1]` plus `{known_points:int,expected_points:int}` on temperature/evidence cards.

| Field | Exact shape |
| --- | --- |
| `status` | `{state: OBSERVED_EXCURSION|NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level:OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of,limitation}` |
| `objective` | `{text:string,policy_low_deg_c:decimal|null,policy_high_deg_c:decimal|null,room_asset_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `temperature_summary` | `{latest_deg_c:decimal|null,latest_at:UTC timestamp|null,coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal},episode_count:int,claim_level:BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of,limitation}` |
| `backup_summary` | `{attempts:int,observed_success:int,observed_failure:int,unknown:int,latest_chain_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `causal_strip` | `[{step:GRID|START_REQUEST|START_RESULT|GENERATOR|TRANSFER|BUS|COMPRESSOR|PROBE,state:OBSERVED|MISSING|CONTRADICTORY,at:UTC timestamp|null,evidence_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}]` |
| `findings` | `{count:int,items:[{finding_id:UUID,revision_id:UUID,title:string,claim_level:string,link:string}],as_of,limitation}` |
| `needs_attention` | `[{code:PROBE_GAP|START_FAILURE|TRANSFER_UNKNOWN|RECOMPUTE_PENDING|POLICY_MISSING,text:string,link:string|null,as_of,limitation}]` |
| `consequence_summary` | `{state:UNPRICED|WITHHELD|RECORDED,quantity:{low:decimal|null,high:decimal|null,unit:'degC_min'},amount:{low:decimal|null,high:decimal|null,currency:string|null},overlap_group:string|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `capabilities[].claim` | `TEMPERATURE_POINT|EXCURSION_BOUNDS|BACKUP_START_RESULT|TRANSFER_POSITION|COOLING_RESPONSE|PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`; `state` = `SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`; `required_roles,missing_roles` = `string[]`; `coverage` = `{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal}`. |
| Foundation states | `readiness.state=READY_TO_REPORT|CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE`; `commissioning.state=CONFIRMED|UNCONFIRMED`; `contact.state=RECENT|STALE|NEVER`; `messages` are typed role-specific strings above. |

## 6. Selected reads and refusals

All routes are tenant-authenticated Site-code reads. Except Foundation/replay, require UTC half-open `from,to`, max 31 days, optional UTC `known_at` (default latest committed watermark); a historical cutoff is labelled historical. Common response `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. Watermark pins latest included S2 decision time + assessment revision; lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, default limit 50/max 200, `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared error `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/Lab gate/source-gated detail, 404 missing/wrong Site type or record, 409 unresolved selected revision or conflicting current recompute, 422 bad parameter/window/selector, 503 ledger/projection unavailable. Valid evidence gaps return 200 with UNKNOWN/null and reasons. No endpoint returns private forcing/oracle, customer/vehicle or a fabricated zero (`.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:83-107`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:54-59,157-161`).

| Route / slice | Additional fields and selection |
| --- | --- |

| `GET /api/sites/{code}/ev/evidence` and `.../evidence/analysis` / 07 | Summary `capabilities[{claim,state,required_roles,missing_roles,coverage}],sources[{id,role,contact,fact_age}],links`; Analysis paged `accepted_records[{evidence_id,family,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,session_id,exclusion}],claim_rules,source_provenance`. W2-00 `/evidence/records` remains exact source link. |
| `GET /api/sites/{code}/ev/financials` and `.../financial-assumptions` / 07 | `consequences[{revision_id,state,quantity_kwh,tariff_revision_id,tariff_value,currency,amount,reason,source_gate,overlap_group,exact_basis}],not_included:[lost_sales,collections,margin,repair]`; assumptions `items[{key,revision_id,value,unit,currency,valid_from,valid_to,recorded_at,basis,source}],missing_keys`. Existing assumption publication route accepts new key with same authorization/idempotency rules. |

## 7. EV screen mapping and owner flag

**Owner flag:** v6.9 EV extrapolations `ev-foundation`, `ev-readiness`, `ev-sld`, `ev-finding`, `ev-findings`, `ev-evidence`, `ev-evidence-analysis`, and `ev-financials` are content references (`frontend/mockups/v69/screen-manifest.json:1095-1645`), not pixel authority. Use W1 Site shell, cards/charts, Finding timeline, Readiness strip, SLD, Financial bridge and contextual Analysis at 1440/390; exact IDs live in labelled detail. Owner confirmation of EV visual authority remains pending. The mock `energy` and `availability` screens are not built in W2; Overview's bounded energy field is not a standalone Energy screen.

| EV screen / slice | Existing v6.9 pattern â†’ read fields | Honest visible gap |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `ev-foundation` / 05a | `foundation` physical Site + interpretation â†’ `feed,chargers,connectors,bindings`; W1 Foundation cards. | Undeclared hardware/limit is unknown. |
| `ev-readiness` / 05a | `readiness` setup and connected sources â†’ `readiness,contact,commissioning`; W1 Readiness strip. | Configured is not commissioned; no contact is not fault. |
| `ev-sld` / 05a | `sld` diagram and part roles â†’ `topology,bindings`; W1 SLD renderer. | No inferred live power flow or battery. |
| `sessions` / 06 | `service` delivered service + `dispatch` selected records â†’ `session_counts,state,supported_kwh,unknown_meter_seconds,events`; W1 Performance chart/list and Analysis link. | Missing end remains incomplete; partial kWh is labelled; no availability percentage. |
| `ev-finding` / 06 | `finding` bounded conclusion + operational context â†’ fault-abort Finding detail and exact evidence. | Fault cause, unmet demand and Action unknown. |
| `ev` / 07 | `site` status/objective/cards/causal strip â†’ Overview counts, energy, site-power context and links. | Below-limit import does not prove cause or charger health. |

The `sessions` mock example combines 10 complete/1 aborted/1 incomplete with 120 kWh and $36; show only replay-derived counts, intervals and tariff amounts. ## 8. Slice fixtures, replay and vendor peer

### Writer, trigger and transaction contract

The 0022 generalization is mandatory before 0025. For `ev_charging`, `analytic_run.cohort_status='NOT_APPLICABLE'`, `cohort=[]`, `cohort_sha256=sha256(canonical([]))`; the complete Finding/Revision NOT NULL mapping and `'mini_grid'` keyed CHECKs in the cold design apply. EV FindingRevision `episode_start=START source_time`, `episode_end=ABORT source_time`; its three dispatch minute columns are null. `support_sha256` hashes sorted exact START/FAULT/ABORT evidence ID+SHA pairs, `cited_evidence_sha256` hashes the exact cited accepted set, and `input_evidence_sha256=analytic_run.input_sha256`. No fault-abort Finding is created before all three accepted facts exist.

Rule 8 `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1`: add `ev_session,ev_session_revision,ev_session_evidence,ev_meter_interval` to `$assessTables` before `$assessInserts` is derived; add `ev_consequence_revision,ev_consequence_session,ev_consequence_evidence` to `$consequenceTables`, then add `ev_consequence_work,ev_consequence_work_attempt` to the same `$consequenceTables` array. `postgres_assessment_store.py` exclusively inserts the four session tables, `postgres_finding_store.py` exclusively inserts Findings, and `postgres_consequence_store.py` exclusively inserts the three EV consequence tables and appends work attempts. Migration triggers alone insert work; the consequence writer appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt atomically with consequence/links or a `FAILED` attempt with error code. Work and attempts are append-only; runtime work INSERT, UPDATE and DELETE are rejected. Cross-writer checks cover `$consequenceTables`. `assessment_worker.py` owns one caller-supplied transaction containing analytic run, assessment, session revision/links, FindingRevision/links and job completion. `financials/recompute.py` owns a separate transaction containing consequence/links and successful work-attempt append. No SQL writer enters HTTP/domain modules and no `DO UPDATE` allowlist entry is added. The 0020 message-only conflict target, per-ordinal savepoint/reread precedence and Receipt V2 classification are inherited unchanged. `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` adds permitted owner INSERT and work-attempt append, rejects runtime work INSERT, immutable work/attempt UPDATE or DELETE, foreign owner, immutable UPDATE/DELETE, unlisted `ON CONFLICT` and dynamic SQL; it includes pass/fail probes for both multi-adapter transaction paths. The guard fails when a named module/table is missing.

`0026` adds append-only `ev_consequence_work` (`id uuid PK`, tenant/site, window_start/end, dirty_basis_sha256, created_at; UNIQUE `(site_id,window_start,window_end,dirty_basis_sha256)`) and `ev_consequence_work_attempt` (`id uuid PK`, tenant/site, work_id scoped FK, attempt_no int>0, outcome `SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, error_code nullable, started_at, finished_at; UNIQUE `(work_id,attempt_no)`, partial unique successful attempt per work). Migration triggers insert dirty work on appended EV session/assessment revision, accepted late EV meter/event, tariff publication, and source-gate revision. Duplicate dirtiness uses `DO NOTHING` on the unique key inside the trigger, not a runtime writer. `postgres_consequence_store.py` selects unfinished work lacking a successful attempt, takes an advisory transaction lock per work ID, writes a Site window revision and links, and appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt atomically; failure appends `FAILED` and remains retryable. `ev_consequence_revision.revision_no` is `1+max(revision_no)` for the locked `(tenant,site,window_start,window_end)` lineage, guarded by unique `(site_id,window_start,window_end,revision_no)` and retried on collision. `overlap_group='ev:'+sha256(canonical(tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,tariff_revision_id))`, using null tariff ID text `NO_TARIFF` for unpriced revisions.

### Response vocabulary

Use the cold design's common `currentness.state`, `source_provenance`, `limitations`, UUID/time/decimal JSON types and exact envelope. EV `start_bounds,end_bounds` are `{earliest:UTC timestamp|null,latest:UTC timestamp|null}`. Session `reason_codes` is an array drawn only from `MISSING_START|MISSING_TERMINAL|CONFLICTING_TERMINAL|CONFLICTING_START|CONNECTOR_MISMATCH|METER_GAP|METER_OVERLAP|METER_INVALID|CLOCK_UNATTESTED|SOURCE_UNVERIFIED|PENDING_RECOMPUTE`. `links` is `{self:string|null,evidence:string|null,analysis:string|null,finding:string|null,financials:string|null}`; null means no supported destination. Foundation `readiness.state=READY_TO_REPORT|CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE`, `commissioning.state=CONFIRMED|UNCONFIRMED`, `contact.state=RECENT|STALE|NEVER`; `messages` uses exactly the codes in Â§2. Capability `state=SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY` and `claim=SESSION_OUTCOME|DELIVERED_ENERGY|FAULT_ASSOCIATION|SERVICE_STATE|SITE_POWER_CONTEXT|POWER_CONSTRAINT|GROSS_TARIFF_SCENARIO`; `required_roles,missing_roles:string[]`, `coverage={known_seconds:int,expected_seconds:int,ratio:decimal}`. `site_power.coverage` has that same shape; `claim_limit` is a string drawn from `CONTEXT_ONLY|REQUEST_AND_LIMIT_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN`.

| Named EV field | Exact shape |
| --- | --- |
| `status` | `{state:OBSERVED_SESSIONS|NO_SESSIONS_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level:OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of:UTC timestamp|null,limitation:string|null}` |
| `objective` | `{text:string,configured_service_window:{from:UTC timestamp,to:UTC timestamp},site_import_limit_kw:decimal|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `findings` | `{count:int,items:[{finding_id:UUID,revision_id:UUID,title:string,claim_level:string,link:string}],as_of,limitation}` |
| `needs_attention` | `[{code:METER_GAP|INCOMPLETE_SESSION|FAULT_ABORT|SOURCE_STALE|RECOMPUTE_PENDING|TARIFF_MISSING,text:string,link:string|null,as_of,limitation}]` |
| `consequence_summary` | `{state:ESTIMATED|UNPRICED|WITHHELD,quantity_kwh:decimal|null,amount:decimal|null,currency:string|null,overlap_group:string|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `source_provenance,limitations` | Same exact common shape as cold; every EV card includes its own `as_of` and `limitation`. |

Canonical manifests in `contract-fixtures/w2/ev/` use `ev-replay-manifest/1`: fixture Site/Foundation/binding/source IDs, UTC window, scenario/profile/model/observation SHAs, seed, expected public envelope/batch SHAs, receipt dispositions, accepted evidence IDs, assessment/consequence basis SHAs and bounded assertions. IDs are fixture constants only. Run twice for byte-identical public replay and duplicate receipts; Stage/Batch/accepted/API bytes exclude private forcing. **Recommendation for the owner: defer `vendor-format-ev-peer/1` to the pilot gate.** W2-05b's registered Lab path and deterministic v4 contract cover the demonstration; an extra parser and cumulative-register fixture cost work without improving the W2 walkthrough. The later pilot contract is OCPP 1.6J JSON `StartTransaction`, `StopTransaction`, `MeterValues`: `timestamp` ISO UTC, `connectorId` integer mapped to registered connector, `transactionId` string scoped session ID, `meterStart/meterStop` Wh integers, `sampledValue.measurand='Energy.Active.Import.Register'`, `unit='Wh'`. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/adapters/ocpp16j_ev_adapter.py` owns raw-to-v4 conversion; adjacent monotone Wh samples become exact kWh deltas by division by 1000. Undeclared reset/rollover refuses `EV_COUNTER_RESET`, absent/ambiguous timestamp `EV_VENDOR_TIME_INVALID`, unknown connector `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH`, unsupported unit `EV_DELTA_UNIT_INVALID`. Raw pilot fixtures belong under `contract-fixtures/w2/ev/vendor-ocpp16j/`. Physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` remain pilot gates. Cold's labelled peer is likewise deferred to the pilot gate (`.agent/architect-W2-design.md:75-81`).

| --- | --- |

No registry node changes until independent slice review and the owner's screen-scope/EV-visual decision. The existing checker therefore remains unchanged.

## Domain seams and claim rules

Reuse tenant/Site identity, immutable time-valid Foundation and binding revisions, source registrations, typed `EvidenceQuery`, S2 AcceptedEvidence/receipt, selected half-open window and knowledge cutoff, revisioned Finding/financial consequence, S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance, W1 Site shell and separate Analysis destination (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:67-84`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:22-54,173-183`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:13-19,4165-4188`). Keep `COLDCHAIN` and `EV_CHARGING` as explicit site types; bind each physical source signal to asset, semantic role, unit, cadence, valid period and mapping/Foundation revision. A signal cannot fill two incompatible roles by label alone. Historical revisions remain inspectable; absent/stale/contradictory roles produce claim-specific Unknown. No status is inferred from gateway contact alone.

| Contract | Cold-chain | EV charging |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Foundation/roles | Chamber/probe and policy low/high Â°C required for excursion; compressor run/power, grid import/status, generator request/state, backup bus or inverter state required **per causal step**. Door, defrost, ambient, load and transfer position optional context whose absence blocks those explanations. Policy and calibration versioned; readings in UTC with Site-local display. | Connector identity and enabled schedule, session ID/start/end/outcome, interval meter kWh, connector status, site import kW and effective site/charger limits. Request/current limit and vehicle demand optional for power-constraint diagnosis; tariff/billing independent. Require declared status cadence and connector schedule for availability, which W2 does not promise. |
| Evidence/assessment | Validated Â°C samples and policy yield crossing interval `[last in-band, first out-of-band]` and return interval; duration/Â°CÂ·min lower/upper bounds, coverage, peak **observed** value. Missing probe segment cannot be bridged. Backup chain matches ordered accepted events within declared timestamp tolerance, reports each missing step and never infers starter-part fault from timing. Distinguish temperature excursion, observed failed automatic start, and unknown cause. | Correlate records by scoped charger/connector/session ID; duplicate/conflicting IDs quarantine. Start + end + accepted metered intervals establish delivered energy; observed fault/stop establishes abort; absent end remains incomplete. Summaries keep complete/aborted/incomplete denominators and known/unknown time. Below-limit site import is context, not proof that the site supply did not constrain a session without synchronized request/limit/voltage evidence. |
| Finding/currentness | Separate excursion and start-response Finding identities/revisions, exact evidence IDs, configuration and model versions; both may cite the same event but no duplicated money. Late evidence queues recomputation and visibly marks previous revision stale until rebuilt. | Session-interruption Finding cites exact fault/stop/meter evidence; incomplete sessions are evidence gaps, not failures. Late end/status can supersede through a new revision; missing data never silently becomes normal. |
| Money | Excursion â†’ unpriced **potential product exposure** unless stock identity, quantity/value and a reviewed product-specific exposure-response rule are present; temperature alone never proves spoilage. Recorded call-out/repair expense needs an actual work/cost record. No summed loss across overlapping backup and excursion Findings. | Metered supported energy Ã— versioned tariff gives gross *scenario* estimate, not collections, margin or lost revenue. Lost revenue requires credible counterfactual demand, price and service baseline; W2 keeps it unpriced. Warranty/repair scenarios wait for asset/work contracts. |
| Readiness/SLD | Reuse S1 configuration readiness and S7 source health separately. Draw declared grid/PV/battery/control bus/generator/compressor/probe links; label inferred electrical-to-cooling relationship as configuration, not live flow. | Draw effective site feed/meter/charger/connector links and configured limits; no stationary battery unless Foundation declares one. SLD monitoring markers derive from bindings + source health, never drawing alone. |

Both analytics consume only normalized AcceptedEvidence with quality, source/measurement/receive times, role and effective configuration. Output stores input digest (accepted IDs/content, mapping/Foundation, window and model version), bounded quantities, reasons and projection watermark. Clock skew, low cadence, gaps and contradiction can independently lower capability. Current-money reads retain LR and S4-05 source gates; `Generated test data` and `Prepared example data` labels are adjacent to material claim limits, mixed/unknown suppress current amounts (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:4165-4188`). W1 plain default with chart/timeline and contextual detail applies from each slice; no raw codes/IDs or simulator terms in the default reading (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:13-15`).

## Simulator-to-evidence contract

1. Add **versioned cold pack** to `simulator/assetops_simulator/packs/`: thermal stock (chamber/ambient heat exchange and load), door-opening heat pulse, compressor on/off and cooling capacity, control power versus compressor power, grid-loss/backup transfer and generator start/failure. Compose with existing electrical mini-grid machinery only at explicit power ports; no event timeline written directly into reports. Declare discrete states/transitions and initialization; step alignment and conservation/temperature sanity guards are executable. Private world has disturbance cause, target trajectory and oracle; public device transform sees only probe, meter, controller and manual-log observations (`simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/model.py:1-110`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/packs/mini_grid.py:1-100`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/publish.py:1-45`).
2. Add **versioned EV pack**: charger/connector availability, requests, negotiated limit, site shared-power cap, session start/energy/end/fault state, meter energy integration. A vehicle-request schedule is private forcing, not accepted demand evidence. Device transform publishes charger events/status, interval meter and site meter separately. Simulated missing end/status is a publication-path loss, not a fabricated `FAILED` event.
3. Extend `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` and registered Lab basis with exact pack/model version, Foundation values, role/source mapping, report cadence, observation bias/gaps, gateway publication schedule and frozen scenario digest; reject unsupported states before Draft. Add `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` and `ev-*.yaml` plus profile versions. Stage seals public envelopes; registered Commit binds run/batch before ordinary gateway release. Continue through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2, S2 receipt and AcceptedEvidence; no direct Finding or evidence seed and no private manifest in product bytes (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:67-75`; `host/gateway_publication.py:1-25`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:131,183`).
4. One deterministic replay manifest per case freezes Site/Foundation/binding IDs, scenario/profile/model/observation versions, UTC window, random seed if used, expected public batch/envelope digests, receipt dispositions, accepted IDs and bounded assessment assertions. Cases per domain: **positive** excursion/start failure and observed EV abort; **negative** in-policy/normal start and completed sessions; **missing** probe/transfer and end/status; **late** delayed accepted reports forcing new revision, with prior read stale. Include contradictory clock/role mapping refusal. Re-run same bytes for idempotent receipts and compare model-independent public physical-adapter peer after typed normalization. A labelled vendor-format fixture is a contract peer, not attested live source; actual physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` remain pilot gates (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-demo-data-plan-proposal.md:3-17`; `frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:79-86`).

# W1 structural fidelity: Architect contract and rework plan (2026-10-17)

Decision: `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. The owner rejected the combined W1 walkthrough; the verbatim verdict is `.agent/owner-W1-walkthrough-verdict-2026-10-17.md`. This is a layout correction, not a data or capability promotion. Scope is the 26 Site screens in the W1 walkthrough and W1 registry list; the registry also labels `lab` W1, but it is not one of the 26 requested screens and needs its own Lab comparison before a later owner session. The registry and its checker stay unchanged.

## Binding layout contract for W1 rework and W2+ UI

For every named screen, render the v69 panel **set, order, titles, relative prominence and size, grid, and visual style** from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, `mockups.css`, `screen-manifest.json`, and `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json`. Use the mock's page heading, compact metric, panel, two-column, timeline, badge, table and gap patterns; port missing tokens/patterns into `frontend/src/ui/tokens.css` and `primitives.css` or shared UI components. Existing type/colour tokens that already match should be reused. Compare the rendered mock at both widths; the JSON titles alone do not define spatial structure. The mock's illustrative values never become product values.

The panel title map below is exhaustive. Default title is exactly `v69-rendered-panels.json`. Approved plain-language changes are: `foundation:2` **Identity and operating obligations** for `Identity & operating obligations`; `dispatch:2` **Selected block Â· {actual selected interval}** for the mock's illustrative `14:20â€“14:35`; `readiness:2` **Gateway status Â· {actual gateway name or â€œNo gateway selectedâ€}** for `GW-MG-01`; `finding-evidence:1` **Evidence bundle Â· {actual Finding name or â€œSelected Findingâ€}** for `F-104`; `sld:1` **{actual site name} Â· {configured topology type, if stated}** for `Kobo Mini-grid Â· AC-coupled mini-grid`. Dynamic titles must use the selected read, never mock fixtures. An unknown dynamic value is labelled unknown in the same panel. No other title substitutions are approved here.

Each mock panel retains its slot when a selected read cannot support its content. Put the precise honest gap **inside that panel** (unavailable, refused, pending, stale, withdrawn, not assessed, absent, or proved zero as appropriate); never synthesize a value, omit the panel, or fill it with a different period's value. A page-level refusal or no-version state may state the refusal before the panel grid, but still renders the panel skeletons with in-panel gaps where the route and screen are valid. Place current app-only content within its owning mock panel or the named contextual Analysis/Evidence/History destination below. Remove the W1-UX leading answer cards and duplicate old panels after merging their supported answer, visible state/scope/as-of, and next action into the matching mock panel. No new leading card. Keep material limits visible beside the claim.

`D-2026-10-16-w1-ux` and `-w1-ux-disclosure` still govern contextual Analysis tabs, narrow exact-reference disclosures, plain words and honest claims. At 390 px the same Analysis destination stays accessible. A closed disclosure cannot hold substantive method or hide a material limit. `D-2026-10-17-w1-rail` and `-w1-ux-06` continue to govern navigation and the configuration/contact/condition distinction.

## Enforcement and review gate

| Gate | Required proof |
| --- | --- |
| Panel structure | Add a route-render test for **each of the 26 screen IDs**, with stocked and meaningful empty/refused variants. Read expected indexed titles from `v69-rendered-panels.json` plus the five approved dynamic/plain-language rules above; query only main-content panel headings, assert exact count and DOM order, and reject any extra leading card/panel before panel 1. Include the selected tab's panels, not hidden tabs/drawers or shell headings. Assert in-panel gap text for unsupported reads. A route with a valid screen identity must not turn into a wholly different error-card layout. |
| Visual evidence | Extend the existing W1 evidence capture tools to save **app and mock side by side** at 1440 and 390 px for every screen, with the same route state and visible panel sequence. Capture one supported and one significant missing/held state per area; log app URL, mock hash, fixture/read selection, revision, viewport, and image paths. Compare top fold, panel widths/heights, grid, density, typography, color, spacing and mobile flow. Where mock sample content differs, judge structure and truthful gap treatment. |
| Independent review | Reviewer checks the structure test, every capture pair and mock CSS patterns. **Any panel set/order/title/grid/prominence mismatch is High**, even if copy tests pass; a leading answer card is High. Unsupported claims and hidden material limits retain their existing severity. No owner session until automated checks and side-by-side review pass for all 26 at both widths; W1 remains unaccepted until the owner accepts the reworked screens. |

The title test is an exact structure guard, not a pixel test. Reviewers must judge relative sizes and visual rhythm from captures. Do not weaken a test to preserve an extra app panel; move its content to the owning slot or contextual destination.

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'
+

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'

+s = s.replace('every core panel at 1440/390 with W1 plain copy and Analysis links.', 'per-route exact panel-structure test for `cold`, `cold-evidence`, `cold-evidence-analysis`, `cold-financials`, `cold-assumptions` in stocked and meaningful empty/refused states, mock/app side-by-side 1440/390 captures, W1 plain copy and Analysis links.')

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'
+

## 2026-10-07

Decision: `D-2026-10-07-s1-foundation-import-and-cutover`. Codex Architect, responding to the S1-01 handoff review under the delegated decision authority in `.ai/ROLE_CONFIG.md`. The controlling detail is [the S1-01 Foundation contract addendum](../frontend/docs/architecture/s1-01-foundation-contract-addendum.md). This records an Architect choice, not owner acceptance of S1-01 or a new owner sign-off on the three Site-first documents. Current project routing treats those documents as implementation authority; their proposal headers remain provenance until an owner acceptance record is identified.

S1 uses PostgreSQL 16 through pinned Docker Compose, psycopg 3, SQLAlchemy Core and Alembic. A separate migrated PostgreSQL test database is required. The first migration enables `btree_gist` and applies the current-valid Foundation exclusion constraint; publication also locks the Site row. No application-only overlap fallback is silently selected. Initial Foundation may exist before bindings, but no acquisition snapshot or evidence is authorized until S1-02 publishes bindings.

Import the Kobo fixture under a configured development tenant, preserving `KOBO-001`, its timezone and v1 source values. Register all ten physical/topological components as assets; retain device inventory in Foundation content. Legacy signal mappings are comparison/candidate data until S1-02 turns them into explicit role bindings. Register one pending simulated gateway and one source per fixture device; one source owns one device report stream and all its signals share the source sequence scope. Gateway credentials remain unprovisioned until the S2 boundary. Internal database IDs are UUIDs; product routes keep the tenant-scoped stable Site code.

Until authentication is built, the backend resolves a fixed configured development tenant; client-supplied tenant identity has no authority. S1 cuts over Foundation only for explicitly imported Sites. The existing YAML Site file is the expected source of that import, identified by path and digest, not a duplicate collision. A changed source or another same-code record conflicts. Product Site index/detail reads select Postgres for registered imported codes (one row) and retain YAML for unimported codes. Legacy product Overview, Evidence and dispatch must not display or write stale file-backed state for an imported code. The Lab currently shares the Site repository and YAML store; it has no separate Site namespace. From import, new Lab Draft setup and existing Draft start/step/run-to-end/Commit for that code are blocked until Lab uses the registered snapshot. The current Stage endpoint is a read-only inspector; it may show historical staged envelopes labelled with their frozen YAML basis but cannot generate or seal more. Commit is blocked even for a stage produced before import. Evidence, Performance and Overview cut over in their later named slices. S1 publishing is battery reserve SOC only; priority controls stay read-only. The Kobo fixture has a 30-minute generator minimum-runtime property but no 35%/90% generator thresholds. An incomplete Foundation gap fixture removes reserve while remaining well-formed under the existing strict Site parser. The Planner owns the API/UI/guard details and records the fixture comparison in the S1-01 packet.

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'

+ 'ev-evidence': [

+  ('evidence.sources[id,role,contact,fact_age],source_provenance', 'Source age is not equipment condition.'),
+  ('evidence.capabilities[].missing_roles,links', 'No ranked next-evidence read: name missing role or “Next evidence not identified”.')],
+ 'ev-evidence-analysis': [
+  ('evidence/analysis.accepted_records[evidence_id,family,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,session_id,exclusion]', 'No accepted record means empty record list; no Lab truth.'),
+  ('evidence/analysis.claim_rules,source_provenance,limitations', 'No selected assessment means “Claim limit not assessed”.')],
+ 'ev-financials': [
+  ('financials.consequences[quantity_kwh,tariff_value,currency,amount,state,reason,exact_basis]', 'No complete supported quantity or valid tariff keeps amount null; gross scenario label stays visible.'),

+}
+
+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'

+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'

+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+
+root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+panels = json.loads((root / 'frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['panels']
+
+# One entry per rendered panel, in DOM order: selected read; in-panel unsupported state.
+cold = {
+ 'cold-foundation': [

+  ('foundation.rooms[].policy,bindings,valid_from/valid_to,readiness.messages', 'No stored operating duty or policy means “Not configured”; no performance claim.')],
+ 'cold-readiness': [
+  ('foundation.readiness,bindings,commissioning.evidence_ids', 'Unbound role or no commissioning record stays incomplete/unconfirmed.'),
+  ('foundation.contact.state/as_of and selected gateway identity', 'No contact is “Never contacted”; actual gateway name comes from selection, never GW-CR-01.'),

+  ('foundation.readiness.messages; evidence.capabilities when W2-04 exists', 'Before capability read or with missing source, confidence is “Not assessed”; contact alone proves no condition.')],
+ 'cold-sld': [
+  ('foundation.topology.nodes/edges,rooms,bindings', 'Mock hybrid solar/battery/grid layout exceeds declared topology: show only configured nodes, with missing parts “Undeclared”; dynamic title names actual Site/type.'),

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'

+def map_section(mapping):
+    out = [
+      'The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.',
+      '',
+      '| Manifest ID / slice | Exact v69 panel → selected read fields inside shared W1-F1 slot | In-panel honest gap / missing read |',
+      '| --- | --- | --- |'
+    ]
+    for screen, entries in mapping.items():
+        expected = panels[screen]
+        assert len(expected) == len(entries), screen
+        for p, (read, gap) in zip(expected, entries):
+            out.append(f"| `{screen}` | **{p['index']} {p['title']}** → `{read}` | {gap} |")
+    return '\n'.join(out) + '\n'
+
+cold_path = root / '.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md'
+s = cold_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+start = s.index('| v6.9 screen and core panels')
+end = s.index('## 7. Slice proof fixtures and acceptance', start)
